FILE: apps/desktop/app.nim

## The Yantra desktop (DESK). Yantra takes the whole screen: an Arc-style sidebar of tabs, pins
## and folders on the left, a Caelestia-style bar on the right, and between them a rounded screen
## inside a frame where windows float or tile. Every window runs one app of the Yantra web
## ("y:<id>") in its own sandbox. Every m3surface is a setting.
## Presentation: v1 retained DSL (stack/canvas/onCanvasDraw/onKey/click); behaviour: state.nim.

import std/[strutils, json, math, sequtils]
import ../../sdk/yantra
include config, tiling, catalog, state

const wallpaper = sksl"""
uniform float2 size;
uniform float time;
float hash(float2 p) { return fract(sin(dot(p, float2(12.9898, 78.233))) * 43758.5453); }
half4 main(float2 fragCoord) {
  float2 uv = fragCoord / size;
  float t = time * 0.02;
  float a = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.9, distance(uv, float2(0.15 + 0.05 * sin(t), 0.95)));
  float b = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.8, distance(uv, float2(0.95, 0.10 + 0.05 * cos(t * 1.3))));
  float c = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.2, distance(uv, float2(0.55, 0.55)));
  float3 base = mix(float3(0.075, 0.070, 0.110), float3(0.045, 0.043, 0.070), uv.y);
  float3 col = base + float3(0.30, 0.28, 0.62) * a * a * 0.55 + float3(0.55, 0.30, 0.48) * b * b * 0.35
             + float3(0.05, 0.05, 0.09) * c;
  col += (hash(fragCoord + fract(time)) - 0.5) * 0.012;
  return half4(half3(col), 1);
}
"""

include ui, settings

setTheme(Theme(name: "Caelestia", dark: true, bg: m3surface, sidebar: m3surface,
               surface: container, surface2: containerHigh, surface3: containerHighest,
               line: outline, text: onSurface, muted: onSurfaceVariant, faint: outline,
               accent: primary, accentText: onPrimary,
               accentSoft: rgba(primary, 0.25), danger: errorRole, success: 0xB5CCBA, radius: 12,
               font: uiFont, mono: monoFont))

needs("ai", "system")

proc framePad(): int = (if screenFrame.get: frameWidth else: 0)

app("Yantra", width = 1280, height = 800, fill = surf(m3surface), net = registryHost):
  onKey: handleKey(lastKey())
  tickClock()
  tickSystem()
  row(grow = 1, alignItems = stretch, fill = surf(m3surface)):
    if showSidebar.get: WideSidebar() else: SlimSidebar()
    stack @screen(grow = 1, marginTop = framePad().px, marginLeft = 0.px,
                  marginRight = (if showBar.get: 0 else: framePad()).px,
                  marginBottom = framePad().px,
                  radius = (if screenFrame.get: screenRadius else: 0), clip = true):
      panel @wallpaper(absolute = true, top = 0, left = 0, right = 0, bottom = 0, shader = wallpaper)
      if deskClock.get:
        column @deskClock(absolute = true, left = 44, bottom = 36, gap = 2.px):
          text "{clock}", size = 104, weight = 200, font = displayFont, color = onSurface
          text "{longDate}", size = 20, weight = 500, font = displayFont, color = onSurfaceVariant
      column(absolute = true, top = 0, left = 0, right = 0, bottom = 0, pad = gapsOut.px):
        stack @area(grow = 1):
          sized aw, ah:
            for w in shownOn(workspace.get).keyed(w.id):
              let id = w.id
              column @tile(absolute = true, left = winRect(id, aw.get, ah.get).x,
                           top = winRect(id, aw.get, ah.get).y, width = winRect(id, aw.get, ah.get).w,
                           height = winRect(id, aw.get, ah.get).h, radius = windowRadius, clip = true,
                           fill = surf(container), border = 1,
                           borderColor = (if focused.get == id: rgba(primary, 0.9) else: rgba(onSurfaceVariant, 0.07)),
                           transition = (if dragId.get == id: 0 else: moveMs).ms,
                           z = (if focused.get == id: 2 else: 1),
                           shadow = (if focused.get == id: shadow(y = 8, blur = 28, color = rgba(0x000000, 0.40))
                                     else: shadow(y = 4, blur = 15, color = rgba(0x000000, 0.25))),
                           enter = pop(scale = 0.92, ms = moveMs),
                           exit = pop(scale = 0.95, ms = effectMs, ease = easeInCubic)):
                if titleMode.get == titleAlways: Caption(w, aw, ah)
                WindowBody(w)
                if titleMode.get == titleOnHover: EdgeCaption(w, aw, ah)
                if focused.get != id:
                  panel(absolute = true, top = 8, left = 0, right = 0, bottom = 0, z = 5, fill = clear):
                    onClick: focusWin(id)
                if not tiled():
                  panel @resize(absolute = true, right = 0, bottom = 0, width = 16.px, height = 16.px, z = 6):
                    onPointerDown: dragBegin(id, true, aw.get, ah.get)
                    onPointerMove: dragMove()
                    onPointerUp: dragEnd()
      if agentOpen.get: AgentPanel()
      if settingsOpen.get: SettingsPanel()
      if spotlight.get: Launcher()
      if dashOpen.get: Dashboard()
      if passportOpen.get: PassportPanel()
      if sessionMenu.get: SessionPanel()
      if controlOpen.get: ControlCenter()
    if showBar.get: Dock()
  if locked.get: LockScreen()

FILE: apps/desktop/tiling.nim

## apps/desktop/tiling.nim
## Hyprland-style Binary Space Partitioning (dwindle), Master-Stack, and Floating
## window manager with per-window StackEdit 3-mode state (Full Preview, Split, Full Code).

import config

type
  LayoutAlgo* = enum
    laDwindle     = 0   ## Hyprland recursive spiral BSP (alternating H/V splits)
    laMasterStack = 1   ## Left master pane + right vertical stack
    laMonocle     = 2   ## Fullscreen focused window within workspace

  TileRect* = object
    x*, y*, w*, h*: float32

  WindowTile* = object
    id*: int
    appId*: string
    title*: string
    workspace*: int           ## 1..9
    groupId*: int             ## 0 = ungrouped, >0 = tab group ID
    owner*: string            ## "user" | "agent" | "other_agent" (for BrowserOS MCP parity)
    viewMode*: ViewMode       ## vmFullPreview | vmSplit | vmFullCode
    splitRatio*: float32      ## Editor/Preview split ratio in vmSplit (default 0.50)
    floating*: bool
    fullscreen*: bool
    minimized*: bool
    headless*: bool           ## Supported for MCP `windows(action="open", headless=true)`
    floatRect*: TileRect      ## Used when `floating == true`
    computedRect*: TileRect   ## Output of `computeWorkspaceLayout`

  TabGroup* = object
    id*: int
    title*: string
    color*: TabGroupColor
    collapsed*: bool
    workspace*: int

proc insetRect*(r: TileRect; pad: float32): TileRect {.inline.} =
  TileRect(
    x: r.x + pad,
    y: r.y + pad,
    w: max(40.0'f32, r.w - pad * 2.0'f32),
    h: max(40.0'f32, r.h - pad * 2.0'f32)
  )

proc computeDwindleRecursive(indices: seq[int]; tiles: var seq[WindowTile];
                             bounds: TileRect; depth: int; gap: float32;
                             masterRatio: float32) =
  if indices.len == 0:
    return
  if indices.len == 1:
    tiles[indices[0]].computedRect = insetRect(bounds, gap * 0.5'f32)
    return

  let splitHoriz = (depth mod 2) == 0
  let ratio = if depth == 0: masterRatio else: 0.5'f32

  if splitHoriz:
    let w1 = bounds.w * ratio
    let leftBounds  = TileRect(x: bounds.x, y: bounds.y, w: w1, h: bounds.h)
    let rightBounds = TileRect(x: bounds.x + w1, y: bounds.y, w: bounds.w - w1, h: bounds.h)
    tiles[indices[0]].computedRect = insetRect(leftBounds, gap * 0.5'f32)
    computeDwindleRecursive(indices[1 .. ^1], tiles, rightBounds, depth + 1, gap, masterRatio)
  else:
    let h1 = bounds.h * ratio
    let topBounds = TileRect(x: bounds.x, y: bounds.y, w: bounds.w, h: h1)
    let botBounds = TileRect(x: bounds.x, y: bounds.y + h1, w: bounds.w, h: bounds.h - h1)
    tiles[indices[0]].computedRect = insetRect(topBounds, gap * 0.5'f32)
    computeDwindleRecursive(indices[1 .. ^1], tiles, botBounds, depth + 1, gap, masterRatio)

proc computeWorkspaceLayout*(tiles: var seq[WindowTile]; groups: seq[TabGroup];
                             workspace: int; focusedTileId: int;
                             stageBounds: TileRect; algo: LayoutAlgo;
                             cfg: DesktopConfig) =
  let inner = insetRect(stageBounds, cfg.gapsOut)
  var tiledIndices: seq[int] = @[]

  for i in 0 ..< tiles.len:
    if tiles[i].workspace != workspace or tiles[i].minimized or tiles[i].headless:
      tiles[i].computedRect = TileRect(x: -9999, y: -9999, w: 0, h: 0)
      continue

    var groupCollapsed = false
    if tiles[i].groupId > 0:
      for g in groups:
        if g.id == tiles[i].groupId and g.collapsed:
          groupCollapsed = true
          break
    if groupCollapsed and tiles[i].id != focusedTileId:
      tiles[i].computedRect = TileRect(x: -9999, y: -9999, w: 0, h: 0)
      continue

    if tiles[i].fullscreen:
      tiles[i].computedRect = inner
    elif tiles[i].floating:
      tiles[i].computedRect = tiles[i].floatRect
    else:
      tiledIndices.add(i)

  if tiledIndices.len == 0:
    return

  case algo
  of laMonocle:
    for idx in tiledIndices:
      if tiles[idx].id == focusedTileId or tiledIndices.len == 1:
        tiles[idx].computedRect = inner
      else:
        tiles[idx].computedRect = TileRect(x: -9999, y: -9999, w: 0, h: 0)

  of laMasterStack:
    if tiledIndices.len == 1:
      tiles[tiledIndices[0]].computedRect = insetRect(inner, cfg.gapsIn * 0.5'f32)
    else:
      let mw = inner.w * cfg.masterRatio
      let masterRect = TileRect(x: inner.x, y: inner.y, w: mw, h: inner.h)
      tiles[tiledIndices[0]].computedRect = insetRect(masterRect, cfg.gapsIn * 0.5'f32)
      let stackCount = tiledIndices.len - 1
      let sh = inner.h / float32(stackCount)
      for s in 0 ..< stackCount:
        let sr = TileRect(
          x: inner.x + mw,
          y: inner.y + float32(s) * sh,
          w: inner.w - mw,
          h: sh
        )
        tiles[tiledIndices[s + 1]].computedRect = insetRect(sr, cfg.gapsIn * 0.5'f32)

  of laDwindle:
    computeDwindleRecursive(tiledIndices, tiles, inner, 0, cfg.gapsIn, cfg.masterRatio)

# ---- Opus DESK geometry -----------------------------------------------------
# state.nim works with plain Rect values + a dwindle that returns them.

type
  Rect* = object
    x*, y*, w*, h*: float32

proc dwindle*(area: Rect; n: int; gap: float32): seq[Rect] =
  ## Binary-space-partition the area into `n` tiles, alternating splits.
  if n <= 0: return @[]
  if n == 1:
    let g = gap * 0.5'f32
    return @[Rect(x: area.x + g, y: area.y + g,
                  w: max(40.0'f32, area.w - gap), h: max(40.0'f32, area.h - gap))]
  var rects = dwindle(area, n - 1, gap)
  let last = rects[^1]
  let horiz = (n mod 2) == 0
  if horiz:
    let w1 = last.w * 0.5'f32
    rects[^1] = Rect(x: last.x, y: last.y, w: w1 - gap * 0.5'f32, h: last.h)
    rects.add Rect(x: last.x + w1 + gap * 0.5'f32, y: last.y,
                   w: last.w - w1 - gap * 0.5'f32, h: last.h)
  else:
    let h1 = last.h * 0.5'f32
    rects[^1] = Rect(x: last.x, y: last.y, w: last.w, h: h1 - gap * 0.5'f32)
    rects.add Rect(x: last.x, y: last.y + h1 + gap * 0.5'f32,
                   w: last.w, h: last.h - h1 - gap * 0.5'f32)
  rects


================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/catalog.nim

## apps/desktop/catalog.nim
## Registry of the 10 working Yantra OS apps (7 real + editor + files + shell)
## with procedural Skia mini-illustrations and a new-SDK starter template.
##
## Reconciled 2026-11: the bundle's 20-app catalog listed phantom ids with no
## apps/<id>/app.nim (daw, drive, vj, ...). They are DROPPED here — spawning one
## would only ever show the engine's deadEngine placeholder. The type keeps a
## `comingSoon` flag so the store can re-list future apps without a rewrite.

import std/math
import config
import ../../sdk/yantra

type
  AppCategory* = enum
    acAll       = "All"
    acSystem    = "System & IDE"
    acGames     = "Arcade & Chess"
    acCreative  = "Creative & Type"
    acScience   = "Sim & Science"
    acTools     = "Tools & Notes"

  CatalogEntry* = object
    id*: string
    title*: string
    subtitle*: string
    category*: AppCategory
    accent*: uint32
    badge*: string
    nimPath*: string
    featured*: bool
    comingSoon*: bool
    isShell*: bool

const
  CatalogApps*: array[10, CatalogEntry] = [
    CatalogEntry(
      id: "typewriter", title: "Marlowe 44 Typewriter",
      subtitle: "Precision mechanical typing sim with Foley audio",
      category: acCreative, accent: ColGold, badge: "TYPE",
      nimPath: "apps/typewriter/app.nim", featured: true
    ),
    CatalogEntry(
      id: "chess", title: "Grandmaster's Study",
      subtitle: "Full chess analysis board with history scrub",
      category: acGames, accent: ColCyan, badge: "CHESS",
      nimPath: "apps/chess/app.nim", featured: true
    ),
    CatalogEntry(
      id: "dna", title: "Helix DNA",
      subtitle: "Real-time animated double-helix visualization",
      category: acScience, accent: ColEmerald, badge: "HELIX",
      nimPath: "apps/dna/app.nim", featured: true
    ),
    CatalogEntry(
      id: "gallery", title: "Galerie Nocturne",
      subtitle: "Interactive 3D art exhibition environment",
      category: acCreative, accent: ColViolet, badge: "3D ART",
      nimPath: "apps/gallery/app.nim", featured: true
    ),
    CatalogEntry(
      id: "calc", title: "Calculator",
      subtitle: "Clean utility calculator, instant arithmetic",
      category: acTools, accent: ColCyan, badge: "CALC",
      nimPath: "apps/calc/app.nim", featured: false
    ),
    CatalogEntry(
      id: "notes", title: "Sticky Notes",
      subtitle: "Minimalist desktop notepad with persistence",
      category: acTools, accent: ColGold, badge: "NOTES",
      nimPath: "apps/notes/app.nim", featured: false
    ),
    CatalogEntry(
      id: "todo", title: "Reactive Todo",
      subtitle: "Task management with list diffing & filters",
      category: acTools, accent: ColEmerald, badge: "TODO",
      nimPath: "apps/todo/app.nim", featured: false
    ),
    CatalogEntry(
      id: "editor", title: "Yantra Studio IDE",
      subtitle: "Nim Code Editor + Hot Reload + Node Graph",
      category: acSystem, accent: ColGold, badge: "IDE",
      nimPath: "apps/editor/app.nim", featured: true
    ),
    CatalogEntry(
      id: "files", title: "Yantra File Manager",
      subtitle: "Workspace Explorer, Source Inspector & Quick-Launch",
      category: acSystem, accent: ColCyan, badge: "FILES",
      nimPath: "apps/files/app.nim", featured: true
    ),
    CatalogEntry(
      id: "desktop", title: "Celestia Desktop Shell",
      subtitle: "This shell — tiling WM, spotlight & dock",
      category: acSystem, accent: ColViolet, badge: "SHELL",
      nimPath: "apps/desktop/app.nim", featured: false,
      isShell: true
    )
  ]

proc appSource*(entry: CatalogEntry): string =
  ## AppView source for a tab. "y:<id>" resolves to apps/<id>/app.wasm next
  ## to the desktop's own folder; a missing wasm shows the engine's
  ## deadEngine placeholder — never a crash.
  "y:" & entry.id

proc findCatalogApp*(appId: string): CatalogEntry =
  for entry in CatalogApps:
    if entry.id == appId:
      return entry
  CatalogEntry(
    id: appId,
    title: appId,
    subtitle: "Not installed yet",
    category: acSystem,
    accent: ColTextDim,
    badge: "SOON",
    nimPath: "apps/" & appId & "/app.nim",
    featured: false,
    comingSoon: true
  )

proc canLaunch*(entry: CatalogEntry): bool =
  not entry.comingSoon and not entry.isShell

# --- Opus DESK registry (state.nim) -------------------------------------------
# The desktop shell reads this generated-style registry: id/name/icon/tint per
# app. Tints are the Caelestia accents (hand-set; do NOT run apps-index.sh).

type
  CatalogApp* = object
    id*, name*, icon*: string
    tint*: int

const catalog*: seq[CatalogApp] = @[
  CatalogApp(id: "typewriter", name: "Marlowe 44 Typewriter", icon: "command", tint: 0xF4D078),
  CatalogApp(id: "chess", name: "Grandmaster's Study", icon: "grid", tint: 0x6BE6E6),
  CatalogApp(id: "dna", name: "Helix DNA", icon: "activity", tint: 0x5CE0A8),
  CatalogApp(id: "gallery", name: "Galerie Nocturne", icon: "palette", tint: 0xA882FF),
  CatalogApp(id: "calc", name: "Calculator", icon: "calculator", tint: 0x6BE6E6),
  CatalogApp(id: "notes", name: "Notes", icon: "file", tint: 0xF4D078),
  CatalogApp(id: "todo", name: "Todos", icon: "checkSquare", tint: 0x5CE0A8),
  CatalogApp(id: "editor", name: "Yantra Studio IDE", icon: "terminal", tint: 0xF4D078),
  CatalogApp(id: "files", name: "Yantra File Manager", icon: "folder", tint: 0x6BE6E6),
  CatalogApp(id: "desktop", name: "Desktop", icon: "home", tint: 0xA882FF)
]

proc drawCardPreview*(d: var Draw; entry: CatalogEntry; x, y, w, h, phase: float32) =
  d = d.rect(x, y, w, h, 0xFF0A0D18'u32, 6.0'f32)
  let cx = x + w * 0.5'f32
  let cy = y + h * 0.5'f32

  case entry.id
  of "editor":
    for i in 0 .. 4:
      let ly = y + 10.0'f32 + float32(i) * 9.0'f32
      let lw = float32(28 + ((i * 19) mod 45))
      let col = if i mod 2 == 0: ColViolet else: ColCyan
      d = d.rect(x + 12.0'f32, ly, lw, 4.0'f32, col, 2.0'f32)
    d = d.rect(cx + 8.0'f32, y + 12.0'f32, 34.0'f32, 18.0'f32, 0xFF1F2947'u32, 4.0'f32)
    d = d.rect(cx + 52.0'f32, y + 28.0'f32, 34.0'f32, 18.0'f32, 0xFF1F2947'u32, 4.0'f32)
    d = d.line(cx + 42.0'f32, y + 21.0'f32, cx + 52.0'f32, y + 37.0'f32, ColGold, 2.0'f32)

  of "files":
    for i in 0 .. 2:
      let fx = x + 16.0'f32 + float32(i) * 44.0'f32
      d = d.rect(fx, y + 16.0'f32, 34.0'f32, 26.0'f32, 0xFF1E2942'u32, 4.0'f32)
      d = d.rect(fx, y + 13.0'f32, 16.0'f32, 6.0'f32, ColCyan, 2.0'f32)

  of "chess":
    let n = 4
    let cw = (w - 24.0'f32) / float32(n)
    let chh = (h - 16.0'f32) / float32(n)
    for r in 0 ..< n:
      for c in 0 ..< n:
        let col = if (r + c) mod 2 == 0: 0xFFE8DCC8'u32 else: 0xFF4A3B2A'u32
        d = d.rect(x + 12.0'f32 + float32(c) * cw, y + 8.0'f32 + float32(r) * chh,
                   cw, chh, col)

  of "dna":
    for i in 0 .. 11:
      let t = float32(i) / 11.0'f32
      let yy = y + 8.0'f32 + t * (h - 16.0'f32)
      let off = sin(phase * 2.0'f32 + t * 6.2831'f32) * (w * 0.28'f32)
      d = d.circle(cx - off, yy, 3.0'f32, ColCyan)
      d = d.circle(cx + off, yy, 3.0'f32, ColEmerald)
      if i mod 3 == 0:
        d = d.line(cx - off, yy, cx + off, yy, ColTextDim, 1.0'f32)

  of "gallery", "desktop":
    d = d.rect(x + 12.0'f32, y + 8.0'f32, w - 24.0'f32, h - 16.0'f32, 0xFF1F2947'u32, 4.0'f32)
    d = d.rect(x + 18.0'f32, y + 14.0'f32, (w - 36.0'f32) * 0.45'f32, h - 28.0'f32, entry.accent, 2.0'f32)
    d = d.circle(cx + (w - 36.0'f32) * 0.22'f32, cy, 10.0'f32, ColGold)

  of "typewriter":
    d = d.rect(x + 12.0'f32, y + 8.0'f32, w - 24.0'f32, 22.0'f32, 0xFFF5F0E1'u32, 2.0'f32)
    for r in 0 .. 1:
      for c in 0 .. 5:
        d = d.circle(x + 22.0'f32 + float32(c) * 12.0'f32,
                     y + 40.0'f32 + float32(r) * 11.0'f32, 4.0'f32, entry.accent)

  of "calc":
    for r in 0 .. 2:
      for c in 0 .. 3:
        d = d.rect(x + 12.0'f32 + float32(c) * 26.0'f32, y + 8.0'f32 + float32(r) * 14.0'f32,
                   22.0'f32, 11.0'f32, 0xFF1F2947'u32, 2.0'f32)

  of "todo", "notes":
    for i in 0 .. 3:
      let ly = y + 10.0'f32 + float32(i) * 11.0'f32
      if entry.id == "todo":
        d = d.rect(x + 14.0'f32, ly, 7.0'f32, 7.0'f32,
                   (if i < 2: ColEmerald else: 0xFF1F2947'u32), 2.0'f32)
      d = d.rect(x + 26.0'f32, ly + 1.0'f32, w - 44.0'f32 - float32(i * 9), 4.5'f32, ColTextMid, 2.0'f32)

  else:
    d = d.circle(cx, cy, h * 0.30'f32, 0x336BE6E6'u32)
    d = d.circle(cx - 18.0'f32, cy - 6.0'f32, 6.0'f32, entry.accent)
    d = d.circle(cx + 18.0'f32, cy + 8.0'f32, 5.0'f32, ColGold)
    d = d.line(cx - 18.0'f32, cy - 6.0'f32, cx + 18.0'f32, cy + 8.0'f32, ColTextMid, 1.5'f32)

proc defaultStarterAppTemplate*(appId: string): string =
  ## Minimal new-SDK app: compiles clean under tools/build-wasm.sh, opens a
  ## window, paints a canvas every frame, counts clicks and keystrokes.
  result = "import ../../sdk/yantra\n\n" &
    "var clicks: int = 0\n" &
    "var keys: int = 0\n" &
    "var scene: Node\n" &
    "var sceneReady = false\n\n" &
    "proc repaint() =\n" &
    "  if sceneReady:\n" &
    "    var d = Draw()\n" &
    "    d = d.rect(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 1280.0'f32, 800.0'f32, 0xFF0D101B'u32)\n" &
    "    d = d.circle(640.0'f32, 400.0'f32, 72.0'f32 + float32(clicks mod 40), 0xFFF4D078'u32)\n" &
    "    d = d.text(24.0'f32, 24.0'f32, 16.0'f32, 0xFFF5F7FF'u32, \"Live Hot-Reload! Clicks: \" & $clicks & \" Keys: \" & $keys, 700)\n" &
    "    scene.set(pDraw, d.cmds)\n\n" &
    "proc main() =\n" &
    "  let page = window(\"Yantra App - " & appId & "\", 1280, 800)\n" &
    "  let stage = stack(page)\n" &
    "  scene = canvas(stage, 1280, 800)\n" &
    "  sceneReady = true\n" &
    "  scene.addHandler(evPointerDown, proc() =\n" &
    "    inc clicks\n" &
    "    repaint()\n" &
    "    handled())\n" &
    "  page.addHandler(evKey, proc() =\n" &
    "    let k = lastKey()\n" &
    "    if k.ctrl or k.alt: return\n" &
    "    if k.code >= 32 and k.code < 127:\n" &
    "      inc keys\n" &
    "      repaint()\n" &
    "      handled())\n" &
    "  scene.addHandler(evFrame, proc() = repaint())\n" &
    "  repaint()\n\n" &
    "proc yantra_main*() {.exportc: \"yantra_main\".} =\n" &
    "  main()\n"

## apps/desktop/config.nim
## Celestia Shell + Hyprland configuration, bilingual यंत्र / YANTRA emblem renderer,
## freestanding UTF-8 text width estimator, and hand-rolled Nim syntax highlighter.

import std/[strutils, math]
import ../../sdk/yantra

const
  # Celestia Shell Cosmic Palette (0xAARRGGBB for Draw canvas calls)
  ColVoid*        = 0xFF07080E'u32
  ColAbyss*       = 0xFF0B0D17'u32
  ColChrome*      = 0xF20D101B'u32
  ColPanel*       = 0xFF111524'u32
  ColCard*        = 0xFF151A2C'u32
  ColCardHover*   = 0xFF1C233B'u32
  ColElevated*    = 0xFF222A46'u32
  ColBorderDim*   = 0xFF252D4A'u32
  ColBorderSub*   = 0xFF333E64'u32
  ColGold*        = 0xFFF4D078'u32
  ColAmber*       = 0xFFD99B38'u32
  ColCyan*        = 0xFF6BE6E6'u32
  ColViolet*      = 0xFFA882FF'u32
  ColRose*        = 0xFFFF75A0'u32
  ColEmerald*     = 0xFF5CE0A8'u32
  ColRed*         = 0xFFFF6B7B'u32
  ColTextHi*      = 0xFFF5F7FF'u32
  ColTextMid*     = 0xFFB4BFE2'u32
  ColTextDim*     = 0xFF6C789E'u32

  # Layout Metrics
  TopBarH*        = 40.0'f32
  StatusBarH*     = 24.0'f32
  LeftRailW*      = 248.0'f32
  RightDockW*     = 58.0'f32
  TileHeaderH*    = 32.0'f32
  BaselineK*      = 1.0833'f32

type
  ViewMode* = enum
    vmFullPreview = 0   ## 100% live running WASM app (StackEdit Preview mode)
    vmSplit       = 1   ## 50/50 live Nim Monaco Editor + running WASM app
    vmFullCode    = 2   ## 100% full-screen Nim Monaco IDE + n8n Node Graph

  LeftTabMode* = enum
    ltSpaces   = 0      ## Workspaces, Tab Groups & Open Windows
    ltFiles    = 1      ## Integrated File Manager (`apps/*`, `sdk/*`)
    ltApps     = 2      ## Categorized 20-App Store Catalog
    ltNodes    = 3      ## n8n-style Visual Node Pipeline Inspector

  TabGroupColor* = enum
    tgcGold   = 0
    tgcCyan   = 1
    tgcViolet = 2
    tgcRose   = 3
    tgcGreen  = 4
    tgcBlue   = 5
    tgcOrange = 6
    tgcGrey   = 7

  NimTokKind* = enum

  NimToken* = object

proc defaultDesktopConfig*(): DesktopConfig =

    cornerRadius: 10.0'f32,
    masterRatio: 0.54'f32,

proc rnd32*(x: float32): int32 {.inline.} =
  ## Freestanding-safe float32 rounding (avoids C `round` import in WASM).
  int32(if x < 0.0'f32: x - 0.5'f32 else: x + 0.5'f32)

proc clampf*(v, lo, hi: float32): float32 {.inline.} =
  if v < lo: lo elif v > hi: hi else: v

proc utf8CodePoints*(s: string): int =
  ## Counts visible UTF-8 code points so Devanagari (`यंत्र`) and box glyphs measure accurately.
  var i = 0
  while i < s.len:
    let b = uint8(s[i])
    if (b and 0x80'u8) == 0'u8: inc i
    elif (b and 0xE0'u8) == 0xC0'u8: i += 2
    elif (b and 0xF0'u8) == 0xE0'u8: i += 3
    else: i += 4
    inc result

proc mTextW*(s: string; size: float32; mono = false): float32 {.inline.} =
  ## Freestanding-safe text width estimator (requires zero host imports).
  float32(utf8CodePoints(s)) * size * (if mono: 0.58'f32 else: 0.51'f32)

proc drawTextVCenter*(d: var Draw; x, boxY, boxH: float32; text: string;
                      size: float32; color: uint32; bold = false) {.inline.} =
  ## Centers single-line text vertically inside `[boxY, boxY + boxH]` accounting for SkParagraph top-box offset.
  let ty = boxY + (boxH - size * 1.18'f32) * 0.5'f32
  d = d.text(x, ty, size, color, text, if bold: 700 else: 400)

proc drawTextHCenter*(d: var Draw; cx, boxY, boxH: float32; text: string;
                      size: float32; color: uint32; bold = false; mono = false) {.inline.} =
  let tw = mTextW(text, size, mono)
  let ty = boxY + (boxH - size * 1.18'f32) * 0.5'f32
  d = d.text(cx - tw * 0.5'f32, ty, size, color, text, if bold: 700 else: 400)

proc groupColorArgb*(c: TabGroupColor): uint32 =
  case c
  of tgcGold:   ColGold
  of tgcCyan:   ColCyan
  of tgcViolet: ColViolet
  of tgcRose:   ColRose
  of tgcGreen:  ColEmerald
  of tgcBlue:   0xFF5CA8FF'u32
  of tgcOrange: ColAmber
  of tgcGrey:   ColTextDim

proc drawFourPointStar*(d: var Draw; cx, cy, r: float32; color: uint32) =
  ## Draws a crisp 4-point celestial star matching the Yantra logo sparkles.
  d = d.line(cx - r, cy, cx + r, cy, color, 1.6'f32)
  d = d.line(cx, cy - r, cx, cy + r, color, 1.6'f32)
  let d45 = r * 0.42'f32
  d = d.line(cx - d45, cy - d45, cx + d45, cy + d45, color, 1.1'f32)
  d = d.line(cx - d45, cy + d45, cx + d45, cy - d45, color, 1.1'f32)
  d = d.circle(cx, cy, r * 0.22'f32, 0xFFFFFFFF'u32)

proc drawYantraEmblem*(d: var Draw; cx, cy, radius: float32; compact = false) =
  ## Renders the bilingual `यंत्र / YANTRA` cosmic orbital emblem inspired by `image_e35c4b.jpg`.
  d = d.circle(cx, cy, radius * 1.12'f32, 0x14A882FF'u32)
  d = d.circle(cx, cy, radius * 1.02'f32, 0x1E6BE6E6'u32)
  d = d.circle(cx, cy, radius * 0.94'f32, 0xFF090B14'u32)

  let segs = if compact: 24 else: 48
  for ring in 0 .. 2:
    let r = radius * (0.94'f32 - float32(ring) * 0.055'f32)
    let tiltY = if ring == 0: 0.92'f32 elif ring == 1: 0.78'f32 else: 0.86'f32
    let phase = float32(ring) * 0.85'f32
    for i in 0 ..< segs:
      let a0 = (float32(i) / float32(segs)) * 6.2831853'f32 + phase
      let a1 = (float32(i + 1) / float32(segs)) * 6.2831853'f32 + phase
      let x0 = cx + cos(a0) * r
      let y0 = cy + sin(a0) * r * tiltY
      let x1 = cx + cos(a1) * r
      let y1 = cy + sin(a1) * r * tiltY
      let col =
        if (i + ring * 5) mod 3 == 0: ColGold
        elif (i + ring * 5) mod 3 == 1: ColCyan
        else: ColViolet
      let thick = if i mod 4 == 0: 2.2'f32 else: 1.2'f32
      d = d.line(x0, y0, x1, y1, col, thick)

  if compact:
    let fs = radius * 0.88'f32
    drawTextHCenter(d, cx, cy - radius * 0.55'f32, radius * 1.1'f32, "यंत्र", fs, ColGold, true)
    drawFourPointStar(d, cx + radius * 0.25'f32, cy - radius * 0.48'f32, 3.2'f32, ColGold)
  else:
    let hindiSize = radius * 0.56'f32
    let engSize   = radius * 0.145'f32
    drawTextHCenter(d, cx + 1.5'f32, cy - radius * 0.46'f32 + 1.5'f32, hindiSize * 1.2'f32,
                    "यंत्र", hindiSize, 0x885A3808'u32, true)
    drawTextHCenter(d, cx, cy - radius * 0.46'f32, hindiSize * 1.2'f32,
                    "यंत्र", hindiSize, ColGold, true)

    drawFourPointStar(d, cx - radius * 0.04'f32, cy - radius * 0.48'f32, radius * 0.09'f32, ColGold)
    drawFourPointStar(d, cx + radius * 0.14'f32, cy - radius * 0.56'f32, radius * 0.06'f32, ColCyan)

    let ruleY = cy + radius * 0.16'f32
    let ruleW = radius * 0.58'f32
    d = d.line(cx - ruleW, ruleY, cx - 6.0'f32, ruleY, ColAmber, 1.2'f32)
    d = d.line(cx + 6.0'f32, ruleY, cx + ruleW, ruleY, ColAmber, 1.2'f32)
    d = d.circle(cx, ruleY, 3.0'f32, ColGold)

    drawTextHCenter(d, cx, cy + radius * 0.24'f32, engSize * 1.5'f32,
                    "Y A N T R A", engSize, ColGold, true)

proc bakeCelestiaWallpaper*(w, h: int): Layer =
  ## Pre-renders the Celestia cosmic starfield + subtle watermark `यंत्र / YANTRA` emblem into a Layer once.
  result = newLayer(w, h)
  let fw = float32(w)
  let fh = float32(h)
  var d = Draw()
  d = d.rect(0, 0, fw, fh, ColVoid)

  let cx = fw * 0.54'f32
  let cy = fh * 0.48'f32
  d = d.circle(cx, cy, min(fw, fh) * 0.52'f32, 0x0A6BE6E6'u32)
  d = d.circle(cx, cy, min(fw, fh) * 0.38'f32, 0x0CA882FF'u32)
  d = d.circle(cx, cy, min(fw, fh) * 0.24'f32, 0x10F4D078'u32)

  var seed = 0x59414E54'u32
  for i in 0 ..< 110:
    seed = seed * 1664525'u32 + 1013904223'u32
    let sx = float32(seed mod uint32(max(1, w)))
    seed = seed * 1664525'u32 + 1013904223'u32
    let sy = float32(seed mod uint32(max(1, h)))
    let r = if i mod 11 == 0: 1.8'f32 elif i mod 3 == 0: 1.2'f32 else: 0.8'f32
    let col =
      if i mod 7 == 0: 0x88F4D078'u32
      elif i mod 5 == 0: 0x886BE6E6'u32
      elif i mod 9 == 0: 0x88A882FF'u32
      else: 0x55E2E8F0'u32
    d = d.circle(sx, sy, r, col)
    if i mod 19 == 0:
      drawFourPointStar(d, sx, sy, 4.5'f32, 0x66F4D078'u32)

  drawYantraEmblem(d, cx, cy, min(fw, fh) * 0.17'f32, compact = false)
  result.draw(d)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Hand-Rolled Freestanding Nim Syntax Lexer (Zero Regex / Zero Host Imports)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const
  NimKeywords = [
    "proc", "func", "method", "iterator", "macro", "template", "converter",
    "type", "object", "ref", "ptr", "var", "let", "const", "enum", "tuple",
    "distinct", "if", "elif", "else", "case", "of", "when", "while", "for",
    "in", "notin", "break", "continue", "return", "yield", "try", "except",
    "finally", "raise", "defer", "block", "do", "import", "export", "from",
    "include", "mixin", "bind", "using", "and", "or", "not", "xor", "shl",
    "shr", "div", "mod", "is", "isnot", "as", "addr", "cast", "static",
    "nil", "true", "false", "discard"
  ]

  NimTypes = [
    "int", "int8", "int16", "int32", "int64", "uint", "uint8", "uint16",
    "uint32", "uint64", "float", "float32", "float64", "bool", "char",
    "string", "cstring", "pointer", "seq", "array", "openArray", "set",
    "Node", "Draw", "Layer", "AppConfig", "InputState", "Event", "Key"
  ]

proc isIdentStart(c: char): bool {.inline.} =
  (c >= 'a' and c <= 'z') or (c >= 'A' and c <= 'Z') or c == '_'

proc isIdentChar(c: char): bool {.inline.} =
  isIdentStart(c) or (c >= '0' and c <= '9')

proc inWordList(w: string; list: openArray[string]): bool {.inline.} =
  for item in list:
    if w == item: return true
  false

proc tokenizeNimLine*(line: string): seq[NimToken] =
  var i = 0
  let n = line.len
  var prevWasProc = false

  while i < n:
    let c = line[i]
    if c == ' ' or c == '\t':
      let start = i
      while i < n and (line[i] == ' ' or line[i] == '\t'): inc i
      result.add(NimToken(text: line[start ..< i], kind: ntkPlain))
      continue

    if c == '#':
      result.add(NimToken(text: line[i ..< n], kind: ntkComment))
      break

    if c == '{' and i + 1 < n and line[i + 1] == '.':
      let start = i
      i += 2
      while i < n and not (line[i] == '.' and i + 1 < n and line[i + 1] == '}'):
        inc i
      if i + 1 < n: i += 2 else: i = n
      result.add(NimToken(text: line[start ..< i], kind: ntkPragma))
      continue

    if c == '"':
      let start = i
      inc i
      while i < n:
        if line[i] == '\\' and i + 1 < n:
          i += 2
        elif line[i] == '"':
          inc i
          break
        else:
          inc i
      result.add(NimToken(text: line[start ..< i], kind: ntkString))
      continue

    if (c >= '0' and c <= '9'):
      let start = i
      while i < n and ((line[i] >= '0' and line[i] <= '9') or
                       (line[i] >= 'a' and line[i] <= 'f') or
                       (line[i] >= 'A' and line[i] <= 'F') or
                       line[i] == 'x' or line[i] == '_' or line[i] == '.' or
                       line[i] == '\'' or line[i] == 'u' or line[i] == 'i' or line[i] == 'f'):
        inc i
      result.add(NimToken(text: line[start ..< i], kind: ntkNumber))
      continue

    if isIdentStart(c):
      let start = i
      while i < n and isIdentChar(line[i]): inc i
      let word = line[start ..< i]
      let kind =
        if prevWasProc:
          prevWasProc = false
          ntkProc
        elif inWordList(word, NimKeywords):
          if word in ["proc", "func", "template", "macro", "iterator"]:
            prevWasProc = true
          ntkKeyword
        elif inWordList(word, NimTypes) or (word.len > 0 and word[0] >= 'A' and word[0] <= 'Z'):
          ntkType
        else:
          ntkPlain
      result.add(NimToken(text: word, kind: kind))
      continue

    let start = i
    inc i
    result.add(NimToken(text: line[start ..< i], kind: ntkOperator))

proc tokenColor*(k: NimTokKind): uint32 {.inline.} =
  case k
  of ntkKeyword:  ColViolet
  of ntkType:     ColCyan
  of ntkString:   ColEmerald
  of ntkNumber:   ColGold
  of ntkComment:  ColTextDim
  of ntkPragma:   ColRose
  of ntkProc:     ColGold
  of ntkOperator: ColTextMid
  of ntkPlain:    ColTextHi

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Opus Caelestia DESK compat: tokens, metrics, and helpers state.nim needs.
# Values follow the Caelestia dark look (deep indigo, lavender accents).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const
  m3surface*         = 0x14141C
  container*         = 0x1E1E27
  containerLow*      = 0x17171F
  containerLowest*   = 0x101016
  containerHigh*     = 0x262633
  containerHighest*  = 0x2F2F3D
  primary*           = 0xC2C1FF
  primaryContainer*  = 0x4A4458
  onPrimary*         = 0x201A30
  onPrimaryContainer* = 0xE8DEF8
  secondaryContainer* = 0x37333F
  onSecondaryContainer* = 0xE8E0F0
  tertiary*          = 0x7FC4C4
  onSurface*         = 0xE8E8F0
  onSurfaceVariant*  = 0x9A9AA5
  outline*           = 0x3A3A46
  outlineVariant*    = 0x2A2A35
  errorContainer*    = 0x5A1D28
  errorRole*         = 0xFF6B7B
  clear*             = 0x00000000
  stateHover*        = 0x1AFFFFFF
  glassAlpha*        = 0.85
  cardRadius*        = 20
  windowRadius*      = 12
  effectMs*          = 180
  moveMs*            = 240
  uiFont*            = "Segoe UI"
  displayFont*       = "Segoe UI"
  monoFont*          = "Consolas"

  frameWidth*        = 12
  screenRadius*      = 24
  barWidth*          = 56
  sidebarWidth*      = 300
  settingsWidth*     = 400
  titleHeight*       = 36
  gapsOut*           = 12.0'f32
  gapsIn*            = 6.0'f32
  workspaceCount*    = 9
  recentCount*       = 5

  registryHost*      = ""
  registryUrl*       = ""

  modMask*           = 0x0FC3

proc fuzzyScore*(q, name: string): int =
  ## Ordered-subsequence match, best first: prefix > word-start > anywhere.
  if q.len == 0: return 0
  let qq = q.toLowerAscii()
  let nn = name.toLowerAscii()
  if nn.startsWith(qq): return 1000 - qq.len
  for i in 1 ..< nn.len:
    if nn[i - 1] in {' ', '-', '_', '/', '.'} and nn[i .. ^1].startsWith(qq):
      return 500 - qq.len - i
  var qi = 0
  for i in 0 ..< nn.len:
    if nn[i] == qq[qi]:
      inc qi
      if qi == qq.len: return 100 - i
  -1

FILE: apps/desktop/state.nim
