      e.edit(v[0 ..< e.caret] & v[q .. ^1], e.caret)
  of Left:
    if hasSel and not shift: e.moveTo(min(e.anchor, e.caret))
    else: e.moveTo(prevRune(v, e.caret))
  of Right:
    if hasSel and not shift: e.moveTo(max(e.anchor, e.caret))
    elif e.caret < v.len: e.moveTo(e.caret + max(1, v.runeLenAt(e.caret)))
    else: e.moveTo(e.caret)
  of Up:
    if n.kind == K_TEXTAREA: e.moveTo(e.lineMove(-1)) else: e.moveTo(0)
  of Down:
    if n.kind == K_TEXTAREA: e.moveTo(e.lineMove(1)) else: e.moveTo(v.len)
  of Home:
    if n.kind == K_TEXTAREA: e.moveTo(e.lineEdge(false)) else: e.moveTo(0)
  of End:
    if n.kind == K_TEXTAREA: e.moveTo(e.lineEdge(true)) else: e.moveTo(v.len)
  of Enter, KpEnter:
    if n.kind == K_TEXTAREA:
      e.replaceSelection("\n")
    else:
      e.fire(e.focus, E_SUBMIT)
      if e.focus >= 0 and e.s.valid(e.focus):
        e.caret = min(e.caret, e.node(e.focus).sProp(P_VALUE).len)
        e.anchor = e.caret
      else: e.focus = -1
  else: e.dirty = false

proc blockNeighbour(e: Engine; id: int32; dir: int): int32 =
  ## The block field before (dir -1) or after (1) `id` in document order, or -1.
  let i = e.paintOrder.find(id)
  if i < 0: return -1
  var k = i + dir
  while k >= 0 and k < e.paintOrder.len:
    let c = e.paintOrder[k]
    let cn = e.node(c)
    if cn.alive and cn.kind in [K_INPUT, K_TEXTAREA] and cn.flag(P_BLOCK): return c
    k += dir
  -1

proc blockKey(e: Engine; code: int32; shift: bool; v: string): bool =
  ## P5: Up/Down past a block's first/last line go to the block before/after (Shift: select
  ## across them); Enter splits (BlockSplit), Backspace at the start merges (BlockMerge) —
  ## the app edits its seq of blocks; the engine then puts the caret where it belongs.
  ## True if the key was used.
  const
    Backspace = 8'i32
    Enter = 13'i32
    KpEnter = 0x40000058'i32
    Down = 0x40000051'i32
    Up = 0x40000052'i32
  let id = e.focus
  let n = e.node(id)
  let size = n.fontSize(n.kind)
  let wt = n.fontWeight(n.kind)
  let (ox, oy, maxW) = e.textOrigin(id)
  let (cx, cy, ch) = caretAt(v, clamp(e.caret, 0, v.len), size, wt, maxW, n.textStyle)
  case code
  of Up, Down:
    let (_, ly, _) = caretAt(v, v.len, size, wt, maxW, n.textStyle)
    let up = code == Up
    if not ((up and cy < ch * 0.5) or (not up and cy >= ly - ch * 0.5)): return false
    let nb = e.blockNeighbour(id, if up: -1 else: 1)
    if nb < 0: return false
    if shift and e.selA[0] < 0: e.selA = (id, e.anchor)
    elif not shift: e.selA = (-1'i32, 0)
    let nn = e.node(nb)
    let (nox, noy, nmaxW) = e.textOrigin(nb)
    let nv = nn.sProp(P_VALUE)
    let (_, nly, nlh) = caretAt(nv, nv.len, nn.fontSize(nn.kind), nn.fontWeight(nn.kind), nmaxW, nn.textStyle)
    let targetY = if up: nly + nlh / 2 else: nlh / 2      # its last line going up, its first going down
    let pos = textHit(nv, ox + cx - nox, targetY, nn.fontSize(nn.kind), nn.fontWeight(nn.kind), nmaxW, nn.textStyle)
    e.focusOn(nb)
    e.caret = pos
    e.anchor = pos
    if shift: e.selZ = (nb, pos)
    e.keepCaretVisible(nb)
    true
  of Enter, KpEnter:
    if shift or e.s.handlerFor(id, E_BLOCKSPLIT) < 0: return false   # Shift+Enter: a line break in a text area
    e.s.blockCaret = int32(clamp(e.caret, 0, v.len))
    e.selA = (-1'i32, 0)
    e.fire(id, E_BLOCKSPLIT)
    e.ensureLayout()
    let nb = e.blockNeighbour(id, 1)
    if nb >= 0:
      e.focusOn(nb)
      e.caret = 0
      e.anchor = 0
    true
  of Backspace:
    if e.caret != 0 or e.anchor != 0 or e.s.handlerFor(id, E_BLOCKMERGE) < 0: return false
    let prev = e.blockNeighbour(id, -1)
    if prev < 0: return false
    let joinAt = e.node(prev).sProp(P_VALUE).len          # the caret goes where the two meet
    e.s.blockCaret = 0
    e.selA = (-1'i32, 0)
    e.fire(id, E_BLOCKMERGE)
    e.ensureLayout()
    if e.s.valid(prev):
      e.focusOn(prev)
      e.caret = min(joinAt, e.node(prev).sProp(P_VALUE).len)
      e.anchor = e.caret
    true
  else: false

proc preedit*(e: Engine; text: string; cursor = -1) =
  ## An input method's word in progress (IME): shown at the caret, underlined, until the
  ## input method commits it (it then arrives through `typeText`) or clears it ("").
  if e.activeChild in e.children:
    e.children[e.activeChild].preedit(text, cursor)
    return
  if e.focus < 0 or not e.s.valid(e.focus) or not isText(e.node(e.focus).kind):
    e.preeditText = ""
    return
  e.preeditText = text
  e.preeditCursor = cursor
  e.caretOn = true
  e.dirty = true

proc nodeRect*(e: Engine; id: int32): (float32, float32, float32, float32) =
  ## The laid-out (left, top, width, height) of a node, or all zeroes when it has
  ## no Yoga node (a leaf that was never styled, or an id that is not in the tree).
  ## Exposed because layout is the one thing a structural check cannot see: the
  ## mock host in tools/verify-wasm.mjs has no Yoga pass at all, so y_node_size
  ## returns 0 for every node and "did this land where it was supposed to?" is
  ## unanswerable from the node tree alone.
  if id notin e.yoga: return (0'f32, 0'f32, 0'f32, 0'f32)
  let l = e.yoga[id].layout()
  (l.left, l.top, l.width, l.height)

proc caretRect*(e: Engine): (float32, float32, float32, float32) =
  ## The text cursor's place in the window (logical px, the window's own scroll applied):
  ## where an input method puts its candidate list. (0, 0, 0, 0) with no text focus.
  if e.activeChild in e.children:
    let c = e.children[e.activeChild]
    let (x, y, w, h) = c.caretRect
    if w == 0 and h == 0: return (0'f32, 0'f32, 0'f32, 0'f32)
    let b = e.boxes.getOrDefault(e.activeChild)
    return (b.x + x, b.y - e.scrollY + y, w, h)
  if e.focus < 0 or not e.s.valid(e.focus) or not isText(e.node(e.focus).kind): return
  let n = e.node(e.focus)
  let v = n.sProp(P_VALUE)
  let (ox, oy, maxW) = e.textOrigin(e.focus)
  let (cx, cy, ch) = caretAt(v, clamp(e.caret, 0, v.len), n.fontSize(n.kind), n.fontWeight(n.kind), maxW, n.textStyle)
  (ox + cx, oy + cy - e.scrollY, 2'f32, ch)

proc activate*(e: Engine; id: int32) =
  ## What pressing a node does, without a pointer or keyboard: a screen reader's
  ## "click". Text fields take the focus; checkboxes toggle; the rest are clicked.
  if not e.s.valid(id): return
  let n = e.node(id)
  if isText(n.kind):
    e.focusOn(id)
    return
  if n.kind == K_CHECKBOX: e.fire(id, E_CHANGE)
  e.fire(id, E_CLICK)
  e.dirty = true

proc blink*(e: Engine) =
  if e.activeChild in e.children:
    e.children[e.activeChild].blink()
  if e.focus >= 0:
    e.caretOn = not e.caretOn
    e.damageNode(e.focus)

proc takeTimers(e: Engine) =
  ## Timers the app asked for since the last look start counting from now.
  if e.s == nil or e.s.timers.len == 0: return
  for (delay, h) in e.s.timers: e.timers.add (e.now + float64(delay), h)
  e.s.timers.setLen(0)

proc pumpSockets(e: Engine) =
  ## WebSockets (W1): connects what the app opened, sends what it sent (in order, once
  ## open), and gives each event to the socket's handler.
  for (id, url, h) in e.s.wsOpen:
    e.sockets[id] = (openWebSocket(url, allowPlain = isLocalHost(urlHost(url)[1])), h)
  e.s.wsOpen.setLen(0)
  for id in e.s.wsClose:
    if id in e.sockets:
      e.sockets[id][0].close()
      e.sockets.del id
  e.s.wsClose.setLen(0)
  var ids: seq[int32]
  for id in e.sockets.keys: ids.add id
  for id in ids:
    let (w, h) = e.sockets[id]
    for ev in w.poll():
      if ev.kind == wsOpen:            # what was sent before it opened goes now, in order
        var keep: seq[(int32, string)]
        for (sid, data) in e.s.wsSend:
          if sid == id: discard w.send(data) else: keep.add (sid, data)
        e.s.wsSend = keep
      e.s.wsEvent[id] = (int32(ord(ev.kind)), if ev.kind == wsClosed: ev.error else: ev.data)
      try:
        dispatchTo(e.app, h)
      except CatchableError as err:
        e.crashed = err.msg
      e.s.wsEvent.del id
      e.dirtyF = true
      if ev.kind == wsClosed:
        w.close()
        e.sockets.del id
        e.s.wsLive.excl id
        break
  var keep: seq[(int32, string)]
  for (sid, data) in e.s.wsSend:
    if sid in e.sockets and e.sockets[sid][0].isOpen: discard e.sockets[sid][0].send(data)
    elif sid in e.s.wsLive: keep.add (sid, data)
  e.s.wsSend = keep

when not defined(emscripten):
  proc pumpSounds(e: Engine) =
    if e.s == nil or e.s.soundQueue.len == 0: return
    for sc in e.s.soundQueue:
      case sc.op
      of 0:
        if e.app != nil and e.app.path.startsWith("y:") and assetSourceHook != nil:
          # an app from Postgres: its sounds are assets there, decoded from memory
          let d = ma.newDecoderFromMemory(assetSourceHook(e.app.path, sc.path))
          if d != nil: e.sounds[sc.id] = SoundSlot(dec: d, playing: false, vol: 1.0'f32)
        else:
          let full = e.resolveAsset(sc.path)
          if full.len > 0:
            let d = ma.newDecoder(full)
            if d != nil: e.sounds[sc.id] = SoundSlot(dec: d, playing: false, vol: 1.0'f32)
      of 1:
        if sc.id in e.sounds:
          ma.seekToPcmFrame(e.sounds[sc.id].dec, 0)
          e.sounds[sc.id].playing = true
      of 2:
        if sc.id in e.sounds:
          e.sounds[sc.id].playing = false
          ma.seekToPcmFrame(e.sounds[sc.id].dec, 0)
      of 3:
        if sc.id in e.sounds:
          e.sounds[sc.id].vol = clamp(sc.vol, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
      else: discard
    e.s.soundQueue.setLen(0)

  proc pumpAudio*(e: Engine; frames: int): seq[float32] =
    e.pumpSounds()
    result = newSeq[float32](max(frames, 0))
    if frames <= 0: return
    var tmp = newSeq[float32](frames)
    for _, slot in e.sounds.mpairs:
      if slot.playing and slot.dec != nil:
        let got = ma.readPcmFrames(slot.dec, tmp[0].addr, frames)
        if got > 0: ma.mixPcmFrames(result, tmp, got, channels = 1, volume = slot.vol)
        if got < frames: slot.playing = false

  proc closeSounds*(e: Engine) =
    for _, slot in e.sounds.mpairs:
      ma.free(slot.dec)
    e.sounds.clear()

proc pumpNet(e: Engine) =
  ## Starts the requests the app queued (the host already checked them against its
  ## `net` list) and hands finished ones to their handlers.
  when not defined(emscripten): e.pumpSounds()
  if e.s == nil: return
  if e.s.fetchQueue.len == 0 and e.imgPending.len == 0 and e.s.wsOpen.len == 0 and e.sockets.len == 0 and not e.netOpen: return
  if e.s.fetchQueue.len > 0 or e.imgPending.len > 0:
    if not e.netOpen:
      e.net = newFetcher()
      e.netOpen = true
    for q in e.s.fetchQueue:
      let local = isLocalHost(urlHost(q.url)[1])
      e.netReqs[e.net.request(q.meth, q.url, q.headers, q.body, allowHttp = local)] = (q.id, q.handler)
    e.s.fetchQueue.setLen(0)
    for url in e.imgPending:
      let local = isLocalHost(urlHost(url)[1])
      e.imgReqs[e.net.request("GET", url, @[], "", allowHttp = local)] = url
    e.imgPending.setLen(0)
  e.pumpSockets()
  if not e.netOpen or (e.netReqs.len == 0 and e.imgReqs.len == 0): return
  for r in e.net.poll():
    if r.id in e.imgReqs:
      let url = e.imgReqs[r.id]
      e.imgReqs.del r.id
      if r.status >= 200 and r.status < 300 and r.body.len > 0:
        let appName = if e.s.appName.len > 0: e.s.appName else: "app"
        let cachePath = netAssetCachePath(appName, url)
        if cachePath.len > 0:
          try:
            createDir(cachePath.parentDir)
            writeFile(cachePath & ".tmp", r.body)
            moveFile(cachePath & ".tmp", cachePath)
            e.laidOutVersion = -1
            e.dirty = true
          except CatchableError as err:
            e.devNote = err.msg
      continue
    if r.id notin e.netReqs: continue
    let (id, h) = e.netReqs[r.id]
    e.netReqs.del r.id
    e.s.fetchDone[id] = (int32(r.status), if r.status == 0: r.error else: r.body)
    try:
      dispatchTo(e.app, h)
    except CatchableError as err:
      e.crashed = err.msg
    e.s.fetchDone.del id
    e.dirtyF = true

proc mediaPending*(e: Engine): bool =
  ## Images here or in apps inside wait on a background decode (a loop looks for them often).
  if e.imgWaiting.len > 0: return true
  for _, c in e.children:
    if c.mediaPending: return true

proc netPending*(e: Engine): int =
  ## Requests on their way, here and in apps inside (a loop keeps polling while any are).
  if e.s != nil: result = e.netReqs.len + e.s.fetchQueue.len + e.imgReqs.len + e.imgPending.len
  for _, c in e.children: result += c.netPending

proc netActive*(e: Engine): bool =
  ## Anything on the network to look after: requests, or open sockets (the loop polls often).
  if e.netPending > 0 or e.sockets.len > 0: return true
  for _, c in e.children:
    if c.netActive: return true

proc onScreen(e: Engine; id: int32): bool =

  ## Some of the node's box shows in the window (inside its clip).
  if id notin e.boxes: return false
  let b = e.boxes[id]
  let cl = intersect(b, e.clips.getOrDefault(id, b))
  cl.w > 0 and cl.h > 0 and cl.y < e.scrollY + e.height and cl.y + cl.h > e.scrollY

proc syncMedia(e: Engine) =
  ## Video and audio nodes (P3): applies play/pause/seek/loop/volume, decodes live only the
  ## videos that show (at most `maxDecoders`, in document order) and playing audio, pulls
  ## due frames, and sends TimeUpdate, MediaReady, MediaEnd and MediaError.
  if e.crashed.len > 0 or (e.mediaIds.len == 0 and e.players.len == 0): return
  var due: seq[(int32, int32)]
  let budget = if e.maxDecoders > 0: e.maxDecoders else: 4
  var decoding = 0
  let liveMode = e.liveMedia and not e.exporting and not defined(emscripten)
  for id in e.mediaIds:
    if not e.s.valid(id): continue
    let n = e.node(id)
    let p = e.playerFor(id)
    if p.path.len == 0:
      if p.src.len > 0 and not p.errored:
        p.errored = true
        due.add (id, E_MEDIAERROR)
      continue
    if n.kind == K_AUDIO and p.startAt < 0: p.startAt = e.now      # nothing draws it: it starts now
    let loop = n.flag(P_LOOP)
    # play / pause (not set: it plays, as videos always did); play after the end starts over
    let playing = not n.has(P_PLAYING) or n.flag(P_PLAYING)
    if playing == p.paused:
      let wasEnded = p.ended
      if playing:
        if wasEnded: (p.heldPos = 0; p.ended = false; p.lastFire = -1)
        if p.startAt >= 0: p.startAt = e.now - p.heldPos * 1000
        p.paused = false
      else:
        if not p.ended: p.heldPos = e.mediaPos(p, n)
        p.paused = true
      when not defined(emscripten):
        if p.live != nil:
          if playing and wasEnded: p.live.seek(0)
          p.live.setPaused(not playing)
    # seek: each new SeekTo value
    if n.has(P_SEEKTO):
      let v = n.num(P_SEEKTO, 0)
      if not p.seekKnown or v != p.seekSeen:
        p.seekKnown = true
        p.seekSeen = v
        let t = if p.duration > 0: clamp(float64(v), 0, p.duration) else: max(0.0, float64(v))
        p.heldPos = t
        p.ended = false
        p.lastFire = -1
        if not p.paused: p.startAt = e.now - t * 1000
        when not defined(emscripten):
          if p.live != nil: p.live.seek(t)
    # live decoding: the videos that show, within budget, and audio that plays
    when not defined(emscripten):
      let wantLive = liveMode and (if n.kind == K_VIDEO: e.onScreen(id) and decoding < budget
                                   else: not p.paused or p.live != nil)
      if n.kind == K_VIDEO and wantLive: inc decoding
      var ww, wh = 2
      if n.kind == K_VIDEO and id in e.boxes:
        let b = e.boxes[id]
        ww = clamp(int(b.w * e.scale), 2, max(2, p.vw))
        wh = clamp(int(b.h * e.scale), 2, max(2, p.vh))
      let vol = if n.flag(P_MUTED): 0'f32 else: clamp(n.num(P_VOLUME, 1), 0, 1)
      if wantLive and p.live == nil:
        if e.audioOut == 0: e.audioOut = if startAudioOut(): 1 else: -1
        p.live = openPlayer(p.path, ww, wh, audio = e.audioOut == 1)
        if p.live == nil:
          p.path = ""                                  # not playable: say so once
          continue
        let at = e.mediaPos(p, n)
        if at > 0: p.live.seek(at)
        p.live.configure(loop, vol, ww, wh)
        p.live.setPaused(p.paused or p.ended)
        if p.live.hasAudio and e.audioOut == 1: p.slot = addAudioSource(audioPull(), p.live.handle)
      elif not wantLive and p.live != nil:
        p.heldPos = p.pos                              # its clock goes on without it
        if not p.paused: p.startAt = e.now - p.pos * 1000
        e.closeLive(p)
      if p.live != nil:
        p.live.configure(loop, vol, ww, wh)
        let st = p.live.status
        p.pos = st.time
        if st.duration > 0: p.duration = st.duration
        if st.ended and not p.ended and not loop:
          p.ended = true                               # it stays at the end until a seek or a new play
          p.heldPos = p.pos
          due.add (id, E_MEDIAEND)
        if n.kind == K_VIDEO:
          let need = max(ww * wh, p.bw * p.bh)
          if p.buf.len < need: p.buf.setLen(need)
          var fw, fh: int
          if p.live.nextFrame(addr p.buf[0], p.buf.len * 4, fw, fh):
            p.bw = fw
            p.bh = fh
            p.hasFrame = true
            e.damageNode(id)
    if not p.isLive:
      if not p.paused and not p.ended and not loop and p.duration > 0 and p.startAt >= 0 and
         e.rawPos(p) >= p.duration:
        p.ended = true
        p.heldPos = p.duration
        due.add (id, E_MEDIAEND)
      p.pos = e.mediaPos(p, n)
    # the file is open: its length, and its waveform if asked
    let waveN = int(n.num(P_WAVEFORM, 0))
    if not p.ready or waveN != p.waveN:
      p.ready = true
      p.waveN = waveN
      when not defined(emscripten):
        if waveN > 0: e.s.waveforms[id] = waveform(p.path, min(waveN, 4096)).peaks
        else: e.s.waveforms.del id
      due.add (id, E_MEDIAREADY)
    e.s.mediaTime[id] = (float32(p.pos), float32(p.duration))
    if not p.paused and (p.lastFire < 0 or abs(p.pos - p.lastFire) >= 0.25):
      p.lastFire = p.pos
      due.add (id, E_TIMEUPDATE)
    elif p.lastFire < 0:                               # a seek while paused still tells the app
      p.lastFire = p.pos
      due.add (id, E_TIMEUPDATE)
  for (id, ev) in due:
    if e.crashed.len > 0: break
    if e.s.valid(id) and e.s.handlerFor(id, ev) >= 0: e.fire(id, ev)

proc pumpCapture(e: Engine) =
  ## Q2: carries out what the app asked of the microphone and camera (the host checked the
  ## capabilities), and feeds the recording from the microphone's ring.
  when not defined(emscripten):
    for (kind, op, name) in e.s.captureReq:
      let file = if name.len > 0 and validStoreKey(e.s.appName): dataDir() / e.s.appName / name else: ""
      case op
      of 1:
        if e.recorder != nil: (discard e.recorder.finish(); e.recorder = nil)
        var err = ""
        if file.len > 0 and startCapture():
          createDir(file.parentDir)
          e.recorder = openRecorder(file, 48000, 1, err)
        if e.recorder == nil:
          e.s.captureState = -1
          if err.len > 0: e.s.logs.add "recording: " & err
        else:
          e.recPath = file
          e.recStart = e.now
          e.recHeld = 0
          e.s.captureState = 1
      of 2:
        if e.s.captureState == 1: (e.recHeld += e.now - e.recStart; e.s.captureState = 2)
      of 3:
        if e.s.captureState == 2: (e.recStart = e.now; e.s.captureState = 1)
      of 4, 5:
        if e.recorder != nil:
          discard e.recorder.finish()
          e.recorder = nil
          stopCapture()
          if op == 5: (try: removeFile(e.recPath) except CatchableError: discard)
        e.s.captureState = 0
      of 6:
        var shot = false
        for id, p in e.players:                     # the camera preview's last frame
          if p.src.startsWith("camera:") and p.hasFrame and file.len > 0:
            createDir(file.parentDir)
            shot = savePixels(file, addr p.buf[0], p.bw, p.bh)
            break
        if not shot: e.s.captureState = -1
      else: discard
    e.s.captureReq.setLen(0)
    if e.recorder != nil:
      if e.recBuf.len == 0: e.recBuf = newSeq[float32](48000)
      while true:                                   # drain the ring (paused: dropped)
        let n = readCapture(e.recBuf)
        if n <= 0: break
        if e.s.captureState == 1: discard e.recorder.write(e.recBuf, n)
      e.s.captureLevel = if e.s.captureState == 1: captureLevel() else: 0
      e.s.captureMs = int32(e.recHeld + (if e.s.captureState == 1: e.now - e.recStart else: 0))

proc pumpCall(e: Engine) =
  ## Q11: carries out the app's call requests, sends the microphone as Opus, plays what
  ## arrives, and hands signaling / state to the app as CallSignal events.
  when not defined(emscripten):
    for (op, text) in e.s.callReq:
      case op
      of 1:
        if e.call != nil: continue
        let lines = text.split('\n')
        e.call = newCall(lines[0] == "1", lines[1 .. ^1].join("\n"))
        if e.call == nil: continue
        e.callEnc = opusEncoder()
        e.callDec = opusDecoder()
        discard startCapture()
        if e.audioOut == 0: e.audioOut = if startAudioOut(): 1 else: -1
        e.callSlot = if e.audioOut == 1: addAudioSource(playPull(), e.call.handle) else: -1
      of 2, 3:
        if e.call == nil: continue
        let nl = text.find('\n')
        if nl < 0: continue
        if op == 2: discard e.call.setRemote(text[0 ..< nl], text[nl + 1 .. ^1])
        else: discard e.call.addCandidate(text[nl + 1 .. ^1], text[0 ..< nl])
      of 4:
        if e.call != nil:
          if e.callSlot >= 0: removeAudioSource(e.callSlot)
          e.callSlot = -1
          e.call.close()
          e.call = nil
          e.callEnc.close()
          e.callDec.close()
          if e.recorder == nil: stopCapture()
      of 5: e.callMuted = text == "1"
      else: discard
    e.s.callReq.setLen(0)
    if e.call == nil: return
    while true:                                        # signaling and state out
      let ev = e.call.nextEvent
      if ev.len == 0: break
      e.s.workerMsg = ev
      if e.s.handlerFor(0, E_CALLSIGNAL) >= 0: e.fire(0, E_CALLSIGNAL)
      if e.call == nil: return                         # hung up in the handler
    if e.recorder == nil:                              # the microphone, 20 ms at a time (a recording has it otherwise)
      if e.recBuf.len == 0: e.recBuf = newSeq[float32](48000)
      let n = readCapture(e.recBuf)
      if n > 0: e.callMic.add e.recBuf[0 ..< n]
      var pk: array[1500, uint8]
      while e.callMic.len >= 960:
        if e.callMuted: (for i in 0 ..< 960: e.callMic[i] = 0)
        if e.callEnc != nil:
          let bytes = e.callEnc.encode(e.callMic.toOpenArray(0, 959), pk)
          if bytes > 0: discard e.call.sendOpus(pk, bytes, 960)
        e.callMic = e.callMic[960 .. ^1]
      if e.callMic.len > 48000: e.callMic.setLen(0)    # not connected yet: don't pile up
    var inp: array[1500, uint8]
    var pcm: seq[float32]
    while true:                                        # what the other side said
      let n = e.call.nextPacket(inp)
      if n <= 0: break
      if e.callDec != nil:
        let f = e.callDec.decode(inp, n, pcm)
        e.call.playPush(pcm, f)

type DialogCtx = ref object

  e: Engine
  req: PickReq

proc onDialogPicked(ud: pointer; p: cstring) {.cdecl.} =
  if ud == nil: return
  let ctx = cast[DialogCtx](ud); GC_unref(ctx)
  let (e, req) = (ctx.e, ctx.req)
  let path = if p != nil: $p else: ""
  var st = -1'i32; var body = ""
  if not req.save:
    if path.len > 0 and fileExists(path):
      try:
        let f = open(path, fmRead)
        let sz = min(f.getFileSize(), 16 * 1024 * 1024)
        body = newString(sz)
        if sz > 0: discard f.readBuffer(addr body[0], sz)
        f.close(); st = 200'i32
      except CatchableError: st = -1'i32
  elif path.len > 0:
    try: writeFile(path, req.saveData); st = 200'i32
    except CatchableError: st = -1'i32
  e.s.pickDone[req.id] = (st, body)
  try: dispatchTo(e.app, req.handler)
  except CatchableError as err: e.crashed = err.msg
  e.s.pickDone.del req.id; e.dirty = true

proc pumpPick*(e: Engine) =
  if e.s == nil or e.s.pickQueue.len == 0: return
  let q = e.s.pickQueue; e.s.pickQueue.setLen(0)
  for req in q:
    let ctx = DialogCtx(e: e, req: req); GC_ref(ctx)
    if not req.save: showOpenFileDialog(Window(nil), req.filter, onDialogPicked, cast[pointer](ctx))
    else: showSaveFileDialog(Window(nil), req.name, req.filter, onDialogPicked, cast[pointer](ctx))

proc pumpNotify(e: Engine) =
  if e.s == nil or e.s.notifyQueue.len == 0: return
  let q = e.s.notifyQueue; e.s.notifyQueue.setLen(0)
  for req in q:
    showNotification(req.title, req.body)

proc dropFile*(e: Engine; path: string; x, y: float32) =
  if path.len == 0 or not fileExists(path): return
  var body = ""
  try:
    let f = open(path, fmRead)
    let sz = min(f.getFileSize(), 16 * 1024 * 1024)
    body = newString(sz)
    if sz > 0: discard f.readBuffer(addr body[0], sz)
    f.close()
  except CatchableError: return
  e.s.dropData = body
  e.s.dropName = extractFilename(path)
  let (cid, lx, ly) = e.childAt(x, y)
  if cid >= 0 and cid in e.children:
    e.children[cid].dropFile(path, lx, ly)
  else:
    let (_, chain) = e.hitAll(x, y)
    var target = -1'i32
    for i in countdown(chain.high, 0):
      if e.s.handlerFor(chain[i], E_DROP) >= 0:
        target = chain[i]; break
    if target < 0 and e.s.valid(0) and e.s.handlerFor(0, E_DROP) >= 0:
      target = 0
    if target >= 0: e.fire(target, E_DROP)
  e.s.dropData = ""
  e.s.dropName = ""


# --- synthesis (AU1): Synth nodes are voices in the audio device's mix ----------------------

proc syncSynths(e: Engine) =
  ## Voices for the app's Synth nodes, their parameters from the props, gone with the node.
  if e.s == nil or e.synthsAt == e.s.version: return
  e.synthsAt = e.s.version
  var seen: seq[int32]
  for i in 0 ..< e.s.nodes.len:
    let n = e.s.nodes[i]
    if not n.alive or n.kind != K_SYNTH: continue
    let id = int32(i)
    seen.add id
    if id notin e.synths:
      var slot = -1
      let v = newSynth()
      if e.liveMedia:
        if e.audioOut == 0: e.audioOut = if startAudioOut(): 1 else: -1
        if e.audioOut == 1: slot = v.play
      e.synths[id] = (v, slot)
    let v = e.synths[id][0]
    v.set(spWave, n.num(P_WAVE, 0))
    v.set(spFreq, n.num(P_FREQ, 440))
    v.set(spVolume, n.num(P_VOLUME, 0.5))
    v.set(spFilter, n.num(P_FILTERKIND, 0))
    v.set(spCutoff, n.num(P_CUTOFF, 1000))
    v.set(spResonance, n.num(P_RESONANCE, 0.707))
    v.set(spPan, n.num(P_PAN, 0))
    v.set(spAttack, n.num(P_ATTACK, 0.01))
    v.set(spRelease, n.num(P_RELEASE, 0.2))
    v.set(spEcho, n.num(P_ECHO, 0))
    v.set(spEchoMix, n.num(P_ECHOMIX, 0.4))
    v.set(spEchoFeedback, n.num(P_ECHOFEEDBACK, 0.3))
    v.set(spLfo, n.num(P_LFO, 0))
    v.set(spLfoDepth, n.num(P_LFODEPTH, 0.01))
    v.set(spGate, (if not n.has(P_PLAYING) or n.flag(P_PLAYING): 1'f32 else: 0'f32))
  var gone: seq[int32]
  for id in e.synths.keys:
    if id notin seen: gone.add id
  for id in gone:
    let (v, slot) = e.synths[id]
    if slot >= 0: removeAudioSource(slot)   # the device never calls it again: safe to free
    v.free
    e.synths.del id

proc synthSamples*(e: Engine; id: int32; frames: int): seq[float32] =
  ## Synth node `id`'s next `frames` stereo frames (48 kHz), rendered here instead of by the
  ## device — for tests and film export (not while it plays on the device).
  e.syncSynths()
  if id in e.synths: e.synths[id][0].render(result, frames)

proc tick*(e: Engine; ms: float64) =
  ## Sets the clock (ms, any origin, only ever forward) here and in every app inside,
  ## and runs the app's `after` steps that are due, in time order. On a fake clock
  ## (tests, video export) they land on exactly the frame they are due.
  e.takeTimers()
  e.now = max(e.now, ms)
  while e.crashed.len == 0:
    e.takeTimers()
    var best = -1
    for i, (at, _) in e.timers:
      if at <= e.now and (best < 0 or at < e.timers[best][0]): best = i
    if best < 0: break
    let h = e.timers[best][1]
    e.timers.delete(best)
    try:
      dispatchTo(e.app, h)
    except CatchableError as err:
      e.crashed = err.msg
    e.dirtyF = true
  if e.crashed.len == 0: e.pumpNet()
  e.syncSynths()
  if e.crashed.len == 0 and (e.lastFrameAt == 0 or e.now - e.lastFrameAt >= 12):   # ~one per display frame
    e.frameEvent()
  if e.crashed.len == 0: e.stepPhysics()
  if e.tipOn and not e.tipShown and e.now - e.tipSince >= 500:   # rested long enough: show it
    e.tipShown = true
    e.ovDirty = true
    e.dirty = true
  e.syncMedia()
  e.pumpCapture()
  e.pumpCall()
  if e.s != nil and e.s.openReq.len > 0:          # URLs for the system to open (Q8; the host checked them)
    for u in e.s.openReq:
      if openUrlHook != nil: openUrlHook(u)
    e.s.openReq.setLen(0)
  if e.crashed.len == 0 and e.app != nil and e.s.workers.len > 0:   # messages from background workers (Q7)
    try:
      if e.app.pumpWorkers(): e.dirtyF = true
    except CatchableError as err:
      e.crashed = err.msg
  e.stepGlides()
  if e.crashed.len == 0: e.pumpPick()
  if e.crashed.len == 0: e.pumpNotify()

  for _, c in e.children: c.tick(ms)

proc pendingTimers*(e: Engine): int =
  ## `after` steps still waiting (so a loop knows to keep ticking).
  e.takeTimers()
  e.timers.len

proc resize*(e: Engine; w, h: float32) =
  e.width = w
  e.height = h
  e.laidOutVersion = -1
  e.dirty = true

# --- start ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

proc start*(wasmPath: string; width, height: float32; scale = 1'f32; intro = false): Engine =
  ## Loads and runs the app module in its own sandbox; the engine is ready to paint.
  ## If the app traps while starting, the engine shows why instead of failing.
  ## `intro`: nodes on the first screen play their `enter` too (films start from nothing).
  let app = openApp(wasmPath)
  result = Engine(app: app, s: app.state, width: width, height: height, scale: scale,
                  hover: -1, pressed: -1, focus: -1, laidOutVersion: -1, dirtyF: true, fullDamage: true, selA: (-1'i32, 0), selZ: (-1'i32, 0), caretOn: true,
                  appDir: wasmPath.parentDir, activeChild: -1, hoverChild: -1, pressedChild: -1, draggingNode: -1,

                  reducedMotion: getEnv("YANTRA_REDUCED_MOTION") notin ["", "0"])
  result.placedOnce = intro
  result.wireLayers()
  result.wirePhysics()
  result.wireBones()
  result.wireTextMetrics()
  try:
    run(app)
  except CatchableError as err:
    result.crashed = err.msg
    return
  result.layout()

# --- hot reload (roadmap U3/U4) --------------------------------------------------------

proc siteKeys(e: Engine): Table[string, int32] =
  ## Every node made by the DSL in a dev build, by where in the source it came from
  ## plus its turn among nodes from the same place ("notes.nim:72:6#0"). Stable across
  ## builds as long as that code is still there.
  var seen: Table[string, int]
  proc walk(e: Engine; id: int32; seen: var Table[string, int]; keys: var Table[string, int32]) =
    let n = e.node(id)
    if not n.alive: return
    let site = n.sProp(P_SITE)
    if site.len > 0:
      let k = seen.getOrDefault(site, 0)
      seen[site] = k + 1
      keys[site & "#" & $k] = id
    for c in n.children: e.walk(c, seen, keys)
  e.walk(0, seen, result)

proc reload*(e: Engine; wasmPath: string): bool =
  ## Swaps in a new build of this app without losing what the user was doing: its
  ## `state(...)` values, focus, caret and selection, scroll positions, and the apps
  ## running inside its `appView`s all carry over. If the new build fails to start,
  ## the old one keeps running and `devNote` says why.
  var snapshot = ""
  if e.crashed.len == 0 and e.app != nil:
    try: snapshot = e.app.saveState()
    except CatchableError: snapshot = ""
  var byId: Table[int32, string]
  if e.crashed.len == 0 and e.s != nil:
    for k, id in e.siteKeys(): byId[id] = k
  let focusKey = byId.getOrDefault(e.focus, "")
  var scrollsBy, textScrollBy: Table[string, float32]
  for id, v in e.scrolls:
    if id in byId: scrollsBy[byId[id]] = v
  for id, v in e.textScroll:
    if id in byId: textScrollBy[byId[id]] = v
  var childrenBy: Table[string, (Engine, string)]
  for id, c in e.children:
    if id in byId: childrenBy[byId[id]] = (c, e.childSource.getOrDefault(id, ""))
  let app = try: openApp(wasmPath)
            except CatchableError as err:
              e.devNote = err.msg
              e.dirty = true
              return false
  try:
    run(app, snapshot)
  except CatchableError as err:
    e.devNote = "it stopped while starting: " & err.msg
    e.dirty = true
    return false
  # the new build is up: take it
  e.app = app
  e.s = app.state
  e.crashed = ""
  e.devNote = ""
  inc e.reloads
  e.freeYoga()
  e.boxes.clear(); e.clips.clear()
  e.motions.clear(); e.entered.clear(); e.enterAt.clear(); e.loopAt.clear()
  e.exitWatch.clear(); e.ghosts.setLen(0); e.ghostBoxes.clear(); e.ghostClips.clear()
  e.placedOnce = false                 # nothing plays `enter` just because it was rebuilt
  e.hover = -1; e.pressed = -1; e.hoverChain.setLen(0)
  e.undoStack.setLen(0); e.redoStack.setLen(0)
  e.children.clear(); e.childSource.clear()
  if e.netOpen:                        # answers for the old build have nobody to go to
    for cid in e.netReqs.keys: e.net.cancel(cid)
    e.netReqs.clear()
  for _, (w, _) in e.sockets: w.close()
  e.sockets.clear()
  e.scrolls.clear(); e.textScroll.clear()
  let keys = e.siteKeys()
  for k, (child, src) in childrenBy:     # apps in tabs keep running, with their own state
    let id = keys.getOrDefault(k, -1)
    if id >= 0 and e.node(id).kind == K_APPVIEW and e.node(id).sProp(P_SOURCE) == src:
      e.children[id] = child
      e.childSource[id] = src
  for k, v in scrollsBy:
    if k in keys: e.scrolls[keys[k]] = v
  for k, v in textScrollBy:
    if k in keys: e.textScroll[keys[k]] = v
  let hadFocus = e.focus
  e.focus = keys.getOrDefault(focusKey, -1)
  if e.focus >= 0:
    let n = e.node(e.focus).sProp(P_VALUE).len
    e.caret = clamp(e.caret, 0, n)
    e.anchor = clamp(e.anchor, 0, n)
  elif hadFocus >= 0:
    e.activeChild = -1
  if e.activeChild notin e.children: e.activeChild = -1
  if e.hoverChild notin e.children: e.hoverChild = -1
  if e.pressedChild notin e.children: e.pressedChild = -1
  e.laidOutVersion = -1
  e.layout()
  e.dirty = true
  true

proc reloadEverywhere*(e: Engine; wasmPath: string): int =
  ## Reloads every app built from `wasmPath`: this one and any running inside it.
  ## Returns how many were swapped.
  let target = wasmPath.replace('\\', '/').toLowerAscii
  if e.app != nil and e.app.path.replace('\\', '/').toLowerAscii == target:
    if e.reload(wasmPath): inc result
  for _, c in e.children: result += c.reloadEverywhere(wasmPath)
  if result > 0: e.dirty = true

# --- documents (D1) --------------------------------------------------------------------

const ptPerPx* = 72'f32 / 96'f32     ## PDF points per logical pixel: 1280x720 px is a 13.33x7.5 in slide

proc pages*(e: Engine): seq[int32] =
  ## The app's `page` / `slide` nodes, in reading order.
  e.ensureLayout()
  for id in e.paintOrder:
    if e.node(id).alive and e.node(id).kind == K_PAGE: result.add id

proc exportPdf*(e: Engine; path: string; title = ""; author = ""): int =
  ## Writes the app to a PDF: one page per `page`/`slide` node (at its own size), or the
  ## whole window as one page if it has none. The same drawing code as the screen, so
  ## shapes stay vectors and text stays selectable text. Returns the number of pages.
  e.ensureLayout()
  var doc = openPdf(path, (if title.len > 0: title else: e.node(0).sProp(P_TEXT)), author)
  let ps = e.pages
  let savedScroll = e.scrollY
  e.scrollY = 0
  e.exporting = true
  try:
    if ps.len == 0:
      let h = max(e.contentH, e.height)
      let c = doc.beginPage(e.width * ptPerPx, h * ptPerPx)
      c.scale(ptPerPx)
      c.clear(e.node(0).col(P_COLOR, WindowBg))
      for k in e.zSorted(e.node(0).children): e.paintTree(c, k)
      doc.endPage()
    else:
      for id in ps:
        let b = e.boxes[id]
        let c = doc.beginPage(b.w * ptPerPx, b.h * ptPerPx)
        c.scale(ptPerPx)
        c.translate(-b.x, -b.y)
        e.paintTree(c, id)
        doc.endPage()
  finally:
    e.exporting = false
    e.scrollY = savedScroll
    result = doc.pages
    doc.close()

proc windowSizeHint*(e: Engine): (int, int) =
  ## The size the app asked for with `app(..., width = , height = )`.
  let root = e.node(0)
  (int(root.num(P_WIDTH, 800)), int(root.num(P_HEIGHT, 600)))

proc title*(e: Engine): string = e.node(0).sProp(P_TEXT)

# --- OS integration (Q8) -----------------------------------------------------------------------

proc dragRegions*(e: Engine): (seq[array[4, float32]], seq[array[4, float32]]) =
  ## The window's drag regions (nodes with DragRegion) and, inside them, what stays
  ## clickable (interactive nodes), in window points.
  for id in e.paintOrder:
    if id notin e.boxes or not e.node(id).visible: continue
    let b = e.boxes[id]
    let r = [b.x, b.y - e.scrollY, b.w, b.h]
    if e.node(id).flag(P_DRAGREGION): result[0].add r
    elif e.interactive(id): result[1].add r

proc toWindow(e: Engine; ev: int32) =
  if e.crashed.len == 0 and e.s.handlerFor(0, ev) >= 0: e.fire(0, ev)

proc menuCommand*(e: Engine; id: int) =
  ## A menubar item was chosen: MenuCommand on the window.
  e.s.menuCmd = int32(id)
  e.toWindow(E_MENUCOMMAND)

proc systemTheme*(e: Engine; theme: int; accentArgb: uint32) =
  ## The system's light/dark theme and accent (at start and when they change).
  e.s.sysTheme = int32(theme)
  e.s.accent = int32(accentArgb and 0xFFFFFF'u32)
  e.toWindow(E_SYSTEMTHEME)

proc deepLink*(e: Engine; url: string) =
  ## scheme://… was opened (a link, an OAuth redirect): DeepLink on the window.
  e.s.workerMsg = url
  e.toWindow(E_DEEPLINK)

proc spellClick(e: Engine; x, y: float32): bool =
  ## A right click (Q9): on a misspelled word in a field with Spellcheck, the word is
  ## selected and SpellMenu tells the app the word and its suggestions (for its context menu).
  ## True if it was on one (then contextClick does nothing more).
  when not defined(emscripten):
    let id = e.hitTest(x, y)
    if id < 0 or not isText(e.node(id).kind) or not e.node(id).flag(P_SPELLCHECK): return
    let v = e.node(id).sProp(P_VALUE)
    let at = e.textPos(id, x, y + e.scrollY)
    for (a, z) in e.spellOf(id, v):
      if at >= a and at <= z:
        e.focusOn(id)
        e.anchor = a
        e.caret = z
        e.s.spellRange = (int32(a), int32(z))
        e.s.workerMsg = v[a ..< z] & "\t" & suggestions(v[a ..< z]).join("\t")
        if e.s.handlerFor(id, E_SPELLMENU) >= 0: e.fire(id, E_SPELLMENU)
        e.dirty = true
        return true



================================================================
FILE: host/host.nim
================================================================
## Yantra host — loads a WebAssembly app module and runs it.
##
## This is the engine side of the contract in sdk/yantra.nim. It:
##   1. loads an app .wasm produced by tools/build-wasm.sh
##   2. enumerates the module's imports and supplies them (positionally, which is
##      what wasmtime's C API requires)
##   3. calls the app's `yantra_main` export
##   4. collects the UI tree it builds, by CALLBACK
##   5. can dispatch events back into the sandbox
##
## Everything the sandbox can do is listed in the `imports` table below. That is
## the security boundary: an app that tries to call anything else fails to
## instantiate, because the host does not provide it.

import std/[os, strformat, strutils, tables, sequtils, sets, times, json, math]
when defined(emscripten):
  import nimwasmtime/web
else:
  import nimwasmtime
  import nimwasmtime/raw
import tree
import wasmmath
import netrules
export netrules
export tree
import ../render/api
from ../protocol/wire import P_NET
export api


# ===========================================================================
# Additional wasm-c-api bindings.
#
# nimwasmtime does not yet expose module-import enumeration, and the host needs
# it: wasmtime's C API matches imports POSITIONALLY, so the host must ask the
# module what its imports are, in order, rather than assuming. `wasm_name_t` is a
# typedef of `wasm_byte_vec_t` (wasm.h:104), which raw.nim already binds, so only
# the type and two accessors are new.
# ===========================================================================

when not defined(emscripten):
  type
    WasmImporttype* {.importc: "wasm_importtype_t", header: "<wasm.h>",
        incompleteStruct.} = object
    WasmImporttypeVec* {.importc: "wasm_importtype_vec_t", header: "<wasm.h>",
        bycopy.} = object
      size*: csize_t
      data*: ptr ptr WasmImporttype

  # NOTE on the `pointer` parameter below (this is subtle and cost a build):
  # `wasm_module_imports` is wasm-c-api and takes `const wasm_module_t*`, while the
  # module we hold is a `wasmtime_module_t*` — different C types, so strict GCC
  # rejects the call outright. It is still correct: in wasmtime's implementation
  # `wasm_module_t` is either a typedef of `wasmtime_module_t` (identical type) or a
  # wrapper whose module is the first field (offset 0, identical address). Taking
  # `pointer` lets the implicit void* conversion through, and the host PROVES the
  # assumption at runtime by checking the enumerated names against the import table
  # — a wrong pointer would yield garbage names, not a silent pass.
  proc wasmModuleImports*(m: pointer; outp: ptr WasmImporttypeVec) {.
      importc: "wasm_module_imports", header: "<wasm.h>".}
  proc wasmImporttypeName*(t: ptr WasmImporttype): ptr WasmByteVec {.
      importc: "wasm_importtype_name", header: "<wasm.h>".}
  proc wasmImporttypeModule*(t: ptr WasmImporttype): ptr WasmByteVec {.
      importc: "wasm_importtype_module", header: "<wasm.h>".}

# ===========================================================================
# The node tree type now lives in tree.nim — a dependency-free module so the
# translation logic can be tested with neither wasmtime nor Qt. Re-exported
# above, so `import host` still gives callers Node/HostState.
# ===========================================================================

# Globals: one instance at a time. The memory base is re-read per callback (see
# readStr) because the sandbox's bump allocator can grow linear memory, which
# may move it.
# Per OS thread (Q7): a worker instance runs on its own thread with its own store, so the
# "current app" these callbacks read is thread-local.
var
  store {.threadvar.}: Store
  memExport {.threadvar.}: WasmMemory
  memReady {.threadvar.}: bool
  host {.threadvar.}: HostState
  currentCaps {.threadvar.}: HashSet[string]

when defined(emscripten):
  proc memTotal(): int = memSize(memExport)
  proc memCopyOut(p, n: int): string = memRead(memExport, p, n)
  proc memCopyIn(p: int; data: string; n: int) = memWrite(memExport, p, data, n)
else:
  proc memTotal(): int = int(wasmtimeMemoryDataSize(store.ctx, addr memExport))
  proc memCopyOut(p, n: int): string =
    result = newString(n)
    let base = wasmtimeMemoryData(store.ctx, addr memExport)
    copyMem(addr result[0], cast[pointer](cast[uint](base) + uint(p)), n)
  proc memCopyIn(p: int; data: string; n: int) =
    let base = wasmtimeMemoryData(store.ctx, addr memExport)
    copyMem(cast[pointer](cast[uint](base) + uint(p)), unsafeAddr data[0], n)

proc readStr(p: int32; n: int32): string =
  ## Copy `n` bytes out of the sandbox at offset `p`. The bytes are copied
  ## immediately; nothing is retained on the wasm side, so there is no ownership
  ## puzzle. Bounds are checked against the CURRENT memory size.
  if not memReady or p <= 0 or n <= 0: return ""
  if p < 0 or int(p) + int(n) > memTotal(): return ""
  memCopyOut(int(p), int(n))

# ===========================================================================
# Host callbacks. Signature is wasmtime's wasm_func_callback_t.
# ===========================================================================

proc argAt(args: ptr WasmVal; i: int): WasmVal {.inline.} =
  cast[ptr UncheckedArray[WasmVal]](args)[i]

proc setResult(results: ptr WasmVal; i: int; v: WasmVal) {.inline.} =
  cast[ptr UncheckedArray[WasmVal]](results)[i] = v

proc i32Val(v: int32): WasmVal {.inline.} =
  WasmVal(kind: WASM_I32, `of`: WasmValunion(i32: v))

proc cbCreate(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_node_create(kind: i32) -> i32
  let kind = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(host.createNode(kind)))
  nil

proc cbChild(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_node_child(parent: i32, child: i32)
  host.appendChild(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32)
  nil

proc cbInsert(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_node_insert(parent: i32, child: i32, before: i32)
  host.insertBefore(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32)
  nil

proc cbRemove(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_node_remove(node: i32)
  host.removeNode(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32)
  nil

proc writeStr(p: int32; data: string; cap: int32) =
  ## Copies up to `cap` bytes of `data` into the sandbox at offset `p`, bounds-checked.
  if not memReady or p <= 0 or cap <= 0 or data.len == 0: return
  let n = min(int(cap), data.len)
  if int(p) + n > memTotal(): return
  memCopyIn(int(p), data, n)

proc cbGetStr(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_node_get_str(node: i32, prop: i32, buf: i32, cap: i32) -> i32 (full length)
  let node = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  let prop = argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32
  var v = ""
  if host.valid(node): v = host.nodes[int(node)].sProp(prop)
  writeStr(argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32, v, argAt(args, 3).`of`.i32)
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(int32(v.len)))
  nil

proc cbSetI32(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_node_set_i32(node: i32, prop: i32, value: i32)
  let node = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  let prop = argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32
  let value = argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32
  host.setProp(node, prop, Value(kind: vkInt, i: value))
  nil

proc cbSetF32(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_node_set_f32(node: i32, prop: i32, value: f32)
  let node = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  let prop = argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32
  let value = argAt(args, 2).`of`.f32
  host.setProp(node, prop, Value(kind: vkFloat, f: value))
  nil

proc cbSetStr(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_node_set_str(node: i32, prop: i32, data: i32, len: i32)
  let node = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  let prop = argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32
  let data = argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32
  let len = argAt(args, 3).`of`.i32
  when defined(yantraTraceStr):
    echo "set_str node=", node, " prop=", prop, " ptr=", data, " len=", len, " -> '", readStr(data, len), "'"
  host.setProp(node, prop, Value(kind: vkString, s: readStr(data, len)))
  nil

var g_frameDt {.threadvar.}: float32

proc cbGetDt(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## yantra_get_dt() -> f32
  setResult(results, 0, WasmVal(kind: WASM_F32, `of`: WasmValunion(f32: g_frameDt)))
  nil

proc cbOn(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_node_on(node: i32, event: i32, handler: i32)
  let node = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  let event = argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32
  let handler = argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32
  if host.valid(node):
    host.handlers[node] = handler
    host.events[(node, event)] = handler
  nil

proc cbAfter(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_after(ms: i32, handler: i32) — run `handler` once, `ms` from now on the engine's clock.
  host.timers.add (max(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, 0'i32), argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32)
  nil

# --- storage: each app's own saved values (N1) -------------------------------------------

const
  storeMaxValue* = 1 shl 20            ## one value: 1 MB
  storeMaxTotal* = 16 shl 20           ## one app, all values: 16 MB

proc dataDir*(): string =
  ## Where apps keep what they save, one folder each. YANTRA_DATA moves it.
  if getEnv("YANTRA_DATA").len > 0: getEnv("YANTRA_DATA") else: getDataDir() / "yantra" / "data"

proc validStoreKey*(k: string): bool =
  k.len > 0 and k.len <= 128 and k.allCharsInSet({'a'..'z', 'A'..'Z', '0'..'9', '_', '-', '.'}) and k[0] != '.'

proc storePath(app, key: string): string = dataDir() / app / key & ".bin"

proc storeGet*(app, key: string): (bool, string) =
  ## The value an app saved under `key`, if any.
  if not validStoreKey(key) or not validStoreKey(app): return (false, "")
  let p = storePath(app, key)
  if not fileExists(p): return (false, "")
  try: (true, readFile(p)) except IOError: (false, "")

proc storeSet*(app, key, value: string): bool =
  ## Saves a value for an app — only inside its own folder, within its quota, written whole
  ## or not at all.
  if not validStoreKey(key) or not validStoreKey(app) or value.len > storeMaxValue: return false
  let dir = dataDir() / app
  var total = value.len
  if dirExists(dir):
    for f in walkFiles(dir / "*.bin"):
      if f != storePath(app, key): total += int(getFileSize(f))
  if total > storeMaxTotal: return false
  try:
    createDir(dir)
    let p = storePath(app, key)
    writeFile(p & ".tmp", value)
    moveFile(p & ".tmp", p)
    true
  except CatchableError:
    false

proc cbStoreGet(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_store_get(key: i32, keyLen: i32, buf: i32, cap: i32) -> i32: the value's full length
  ## (-1 if none), copying up to `cap` bytes into the app's memory at `buf`.
  let key = readStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32)
  let (found, v) = storeGet(host.appName, key)
  if found and argAt(args, 3).`of`.i32 > 0: writeStr(argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32, v, argAt(args, 3).`of`.i32)
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(if found: int32(v.len) else: -1'i32))
  nil

proc cbStoreSet(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_store_set(key: i32, keyLen: i32, val: i32, valLen: i32) -> i32: 1 saved, 0 refused.
  let key = readStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32)
  let n = argAt(args, 3).`of`.i32
  let v = if n > 0: readStr(argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32, n) else: ""
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(if n >= 0 and v.len == n and storeSet(host.appName, key, v): 1'i32 else: 0'i32))
  nil

# --- network (F1): fetches only to the hosts the app's window lists ------------------------

const fetchMaxPending* = 16

proc windowNet(): string =
  if host.nodes.len > 0 and host.nodes[0].alive and P_NET in host.nodes[0].props:
    let v = host.nodes[0].props[P_NET]
    if v.kind == vkString: return v.s

proc cbFetch(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_fetch(url: i32, urlLen: i32, handler: i32) -> i32: the request's id, 0 if refused
  ## (host not in the window's `net` list, not https, or too many at once). `handler` runs
  ## once when the answer is in; y_fetch_status / y_fetch_body read it then.
  let url = readStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32)
  var id = 0'i32
  if netAllowed(windowNet(), url) and host.fetchQueue.len < fetchMaxPending:
    inc host.nextFetch
    id = host.nextFetch
    host.fetchQueue.add FetchReq(id: id, meth: "GET", url: url, handler: argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32)
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(id))
  nil

const
  fetchMethods = ["GET", "POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE", "HEAD"]
  hostOnlyHeaders = ["host", "content-length", "transfer-encoding", "connection", "upgrade"]
  wsMaxOpen* = 8

proc parseRequest*(req: string): (bool, FetchReq) =
  ## "METHOD\nURL\nName: value\n…\n\nBODY" as the SDK writes it; false if malformed, a
  ## method we do not send, or a header only the host may set.
  let lines = req.split('\n')
  if lines.len < 2 or lines[0] notin fetchMethods: return
  var r = FetchReq(meth: lines[0], url: lines[1])
  var i = 2
  while i < lines.len and lines[i].len > 0:
    let k = lines[i].find(':')
    if k <= 0 or lines[i].contains('\r') or lines[i][0 ..< k].strip.toLowerAscii in hostOnlyHeaders: return
    r.headers.add lines[i]
    inc i
  if i < lines.len: r.body = lines[i + 1 .. ^1].join("\n")
  (true, r)

proc cbFetchEx(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_fetch_ex(req: i32, reqLen: i32, handler: i32) -> i32: like y_fetch, with a method,
  ## headers and a body (parseRequest). 0 if refused.
  let (ok, r) = parseRequest(readStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32))
  var id = 0'i32
  if ok and netAllowed(windowNet(), r.url) and host.fetchQueue.len < fetchMaxPending:
    inc host.nextFetch
    id = host.nextFetch
    var q = r
    q.id = id
    q.handler = argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32
    host.fetchQueue.add q
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(id))
  nil

# ---- the system (y_sys): for the desktop's control centre --------------------------------------
when defined(windows):
  {.compile: "sys_win.c".}
  {.passL: "-lole32 -liphlpapi -lwlanapi -ladvapi32".}
else:
  {.compile: "sys_linux.c".}
proc ysys_battery(): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_charging(): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_volume(): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_set_volume(pct: cint): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_muted(): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_set_muted(on: cint): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_network(): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_wifi_name(buf: cstring; cap: cint): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_uptime(): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_cpu(): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_memory(): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_disk(): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_user(buf: cstring; cap: cint): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_host(buf: cstring; cap: cint): cint {.importc, cdecl.}
proc ysys_os(buf: cstring; cap: cint): cint {.importc, cdecl.}

const sysTextMax* = 128
  ## How much of a text the C shim will ever write (battery/volume have no text; a name is
  ## shorter than this). One byte over: the shims NUL-terminate, so the buffer needs a spare.

proc cbSys(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_sys(op: i32, arg: i32) -> i32, for an app with the `system` grant (else -1): 1 battery
  ## percent, 2 on mains power (1/0), 3 volume 0-100, 4 set the volume to `arg`, 5 muted (1/0),
  ## 6 mute (`arg` 1) or unmute, 7 network: 0 offline, 1 wired, 2 Wi-Fi, 8 seconds since the
  ## machine started, 9 CPU use %, 10 memory in use %, 11 the system disk's use %. -1: not known.
  let arg = argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32
  let v = if "system" notin currentCaps: -1'i32
          else:
            case argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
            of 1: int32(ysys_battery())
            of 2: int32(ysys_charging())
            of 3: int32(ysys_volume())
            of 4: int32(ysys_set_volume(cint(arg)))
            of 5: int32(ysys_muted())
            of 6: int32(ysys_set_muted(cint(arg)))
            of 7: int32(ysys_network())
            of 8: int32(ysys_uptime())
            of 9: int32(ysys_cpu())
            of 10: int32(ysys_memory())
            of 11: int32(ysys_disk())
            else: -1'i32
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(v))
  nil

proc cbSysText(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_sys_text(op: i32, buf: i32, cap: i32) -> i32: a text about the system, its length (-1 if
  ## not known), copying up to `cap` bytes to `buf`. 1: the Wi-Fi network's name, 2: the user's
  ## name, 3: the computer's name, 4: the system's name.
  var n = -1'i32
  let op = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  if "system" in currentCaps and op in 1'i32 .. 4'i32:
    # +1 so the shim's NUL terminator lands inside our own allocation, not past its end.
    var name = newString(sysTextMax + 1)
    let k = case op
            of 1: ysys_wifi_name(cstring(name), sysTextMax)
            of 2: ysys_user(cstring(name), sysTextMax)
            of 3: ysys_host(cstring(name), sysTextMax)
            else: ysys_os(cstring(name), sysTextMax)
    if k >= 0:
      name.setLen(k)
      n = int32(k)
      if argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32 > 0: writeStr(argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32, name, argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32)
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(n))
  nil

# ---- the model (y_ai): the host holds the key, the app never sees it ---------------------------

proc aiBackend*(): int32 =
  ## Which model answers `y_ai`: 2 a local OpenAI-compatible server (YANTRA_AI_URL), 1 Claude
  ## (ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, read from the environment and never stored), 0 none.
  if getEnv("YANTRA_AI_URL").len > 0: 2'i32
  elif getEnv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY").len > 0: 1'i32
  else: 0'i32

proc cbAiReady(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_ai_ready() -> i32: aiBackend() for an app with the `ai` grant, else 0. Honest: a machine
  ## with no model set up reads 0, so an app hides its agent rather than offering a dead button.
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(if "ai" in currentCaps: aiBackend() else: 0'i32))
  nil

proc cbAi(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_ai(req: i32, reqLen: i32, handler: i32) -> i32: asks the model. `req` is JSON
  ## {system, messages: [{role, content}], max_tokens}; the host adds the model and the key (the
  ## app never sees it) and queues it like a fetch: y_fetch_status / y_fetch_body read the
  ## answer in `handler`. 0 if refused: no `ai` grant, no model set up, or too many requests.
  var id = 0'i32
  let backend = aiBackend()
  if "ai" in currentCaps and backend > 0 and host.fetchQueue.len < fetchMaxPending:
    try:
      let j = parseJson(readStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32))
      var q = FetchReq(meth: "POST", handler: argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32)
      if backend == 2:                       # a local server: the OpenAI chat shape
        var msgs = newJArray()
        if j{"system"}.getStr.len > 0: msgs.add %*{"role": "system", "content": j["system"].getStr}
        for m in j{"messages"}: msgs.add m
        q.url = getEnv("YANTRA_AI_URL")
        q.headers = @["content-type: application/json"]
        q.body = $(%*{"model": getEnv("YANTRA_MODEL", "local"), "messages": msgs,
                      "max_tokens": j{"max_tokens"}.getInt(1024)})
      else:
        var body = j
        body["model"] = %getEnv("YANTRA_MODEL", "claude-sonnet-5")
        q.url = "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages"
        q.headers = @["x-api-key: " & getEnv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"),
                      "anthropic-version: 2023-06-01",
                      "content-type: application/json"]
        q.body = $body
      inc host.nextFetch
      id = host.nextFetch
      q.id = id
      host.fetchQueue.add q
    except CatchableError:
      id = 0
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(id))
  nil

proc cbWsOpen(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_ws_open(url: i32, urlLen: i32, handler: i32) -> i32: a WebSocket's id, 0 if refused
  ## (host not in `net`, not wss — ws only to this machine —, or 8 already open). `handler`
  ## runs on every event: y_ws_event says which, y_ws_data gives the message.
  let url = readStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32)
  let scheme = urlHost(url)[0]
  var id = 0'i32
  if scheme in ["ws", "wss"] and netAllowed(windowNet(), url) and host.wsLive.len < wsMaxOpen:
    inc host.nextFetch
    id = host.nextFetch
    host.wsOpen.add (id, url, argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32)
    host.wsLive.incl id
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(id))
  nil

proc cbWsSend(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_ws_send(id: i32, data: i32, len: i32) -> i32: 1 queued (sent in order once open), 0 if
  ## the socket is unknown or closed.
  let id = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  let n = argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32
  var ok = 0'i32
  if id in host.wsLive and n >= 0 and n <= 8 shl 20:
    host.wsSend.add (id, if n > 0: readStr(argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32, n) else: "")
    ok = 1
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(ok))
  nil

proc cbWsClose(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_ws_close(id: i32): closes it; its handler hears nothing more.
  let id = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  if id in host.wsLive:
    host.wsLive.excl id
    host.wsClose.add id
  nil

proc cbWsEvent(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_ws_event(id: i32) -> i32: inside its handler, 1 opened, 2 a message, 3 closed; else 0.
  let id = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(if id in host.wsEvent: host.wsEvent[id][0] else: 0'i32))
  nil

proc cbWsData(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_ws_data(id: i32, buf: i32, cap: i32) -> i32: the message (or why it closed); its full
  ## length, -1 if none, copying up to `cap` bytes to `buf`.
  let id = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  var n = -1'i32
  if id in host.wsEvent:
    let d = host.wsEvent[id][1]
    n = int32(d.len)
    if argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32 > 0: writeStr(argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32, d, argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32)
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(n))
  nil

proc cbFetchStatus(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_fetch_status(id: i32) -> i32: the HTTP status, 0 if it failed (the body is then the
  ## reason), -1 if there is no answer for `id` (only inside its handler).
  let id = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(if id in host.fetchDone: host.fetchDone[id][0] else: -1'i32))
  nil

proc cbFetchBody(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_fetch_body(id: i32, buf: i32, cap: i32) -> i32: the body's full length (-1: none),
  ## copying up to `cap` bytes into the app's memory at `buf`.
  let id = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  var n = -1'i32
  if id in host.fetchDone:
    let body = host.fetchDone[id][1]
    n = int32(body.len)
    if argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32 > 0: writeStr(argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32, body, argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32)
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(n))
  nil

proc cbKey(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_key(which: i32) -> i32: in a Key handler, the key's code (0) or modifier bits (1).
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(if argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32 == 0: host.keyCode else: host.keyMods))
  nil

proc cbKeyHandled(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_key_handled(): the app took the key; it goes no further (no bubbling, no built-in action).
  host.keyHandled = true
  nil

proc cbNodeSize(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_node_size(node: i32, axis: i32) -> f32: the node's laid-out width (axis 0) or height
  ## (1), for nodes with a Resize handler; its scroll offset (2) for nodes with a Scroll
  ## handler. 0 before the first layout / scroll. Video/audio (P3): 3 position and 4 length
  ## in seconds, 5 how many waveform peaks there are, 1000 + i peak i (0..1). 6: the caret
  ## byte a BlockSplit happened at (P5). 7, 8: a canvas pointer event's x, y in the node (P7);
  ## 9: a canvas's horizontal scroll (P8).
  let id = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  let (w, hh) = host.sizes.getOrDefault(id, (0'f32, 0'f32))
  let axis = argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32
  let v = if axis == 0: w elif axis == 1: hh
          elif axis == 3: host.mediaTime.getOrDefault(id, (0'f32, 0'f32))[0]
          elif axis == 4: host.mediaTime.getOrDefault(id, (0'f32, 0'f32))[1]
          elif axis == 5: float32(host.waveforms.getOrDefault(id, @[]).len)
          elif axis == 6: float32(host.blockCaret)
          elif axis == 7: host.pointerAt[0]
          elif axis == 8: host.pointerAt[1]
          elif axis == 9: host.scrollXOf.getOrDefault(id, 0'f32)
          elif axis == 10: float32(host.linkHit)
          elif axis == 11: float32(host.findCount)
          elif axis == 12: float32(host.findAt)
          elif axis == 13: host.pointerPage[0]
          elif axis == 14: host.pointerPage[1]
          elif axis == 15: float32(host.command)
          elif axis == 16: float32(host.menuCmd)
          elif axis == 17: float32(host.sysTheme)
          elif axis == 18: float32(host.accent)
          elif axis == 19: float32(host.spellRange[0])
          elif axis == 20: float32(host.spellRange[1])
          elif axis >= 1000:
            (let wf = host.waveforms.getOrDefault(id, @[]); if axis - 1000 < wf.len: wf[axis - 1000] else: 0'f32)
          else: host.scrollOf.getOrDefault(id, 0'f32)
  setResult(results, 0, WasmVal(kind: WASM_F32, `of`: WasmValunion(f32: v)))
  nil

proc cbLog(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## yantra_log(data: i32, len: i32) — the only output channel an app has.
  host.logs.add readStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32)
  nil

# --- workers (Q7) --------------------------------------------------------------------
# A worker is another instance (of the app's own module or another one from its folder) on
# its own OS thread, with its own wasmtime engine and store. Messages are strings, copied
# through two channels; the parent gets its worker's messages as events in `pumpWorkers`.
when not defined(emscripten):
  type WorkerBox = object
    path: string
    inbox, outbox: Channel[string]   ## to the worker / from it
    thread: Thread[ptr WorkerBox]

  proc callOptional(inst: Instance; name: string): bool =
    let (_, ok) = inst.getFunc(name)
    if ok: callVoid(inst, name)
    ok

  proc loadApp*(wasmPath: string): Instance

  proc workerMain(w: ptr WorkerBox) {.thread.} =
    {.cast(gcsafe).}:                              # only this thread's own (threadvar) app state
      try:
        let inst = loadApp(w.path)
        host.workerOut = addr w.outbox
        currentCaps = initHashSet[string]()         # a worker gets no capabilities
        discard callOptional(inst, "NimMain")        # top-level state; no UI is built
        discard callOptional(inst, "yantra_worker_main")
        while true:
          let m = w.inbox.recv()
          if m == "\0stop": break
          host.workerMsg = m
          discard callOptional(inst, "yantra_worker_message")
      except CatchableError as err:
        w.outbox.send("\0error " & err.msg)

proc cbWorkerStart(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_worker_start(name: i32, len: i32, handler: i32) -> i32: starts module `name` from the
  ## app's folder ("" = the app's own module) as a worker; its messages come to `handler`.
  ## -2 a bad name, -3 no such module, -1 inside a worker (workers don't nest).
  when defined(emscripten):
    setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-1))
  else:
    let name = readStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32)
    let h = argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32
    if host.workerOut != nil: (setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-1)); return nil)
    if name.len > 64 or not name.allCharsInSet({'a'..'z', 'A'..'Z', '0'..'9', '_', '-'}):
      setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-2))
      return nil
    let path = host.appDir / (if name.len > 0: name else: host.appName) & ".wasm"
    if not fileExists(path): (setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-3)); return nil)
    let w = cast[ptr WorkerBox](allocShared0(sizeof(WorkerBox)))
    w.path = path
    w.inbox.open()
    w.outbox.open()
    createThread(w.thread, workerMain, w)
    inc host.nextWorker
    host.workers[host.nextWorker] = (cast[pointer](w), h)
    setResult(results, 0, i32Val(host.nextWorker))
  nil

proc cbWorkerSend(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_worker_send(id: i32, data: i32, len: i32) -> i32: a message to worker `id` (0 ok, -1 none).
  when not defined(emscripten):
    let id = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
    if id notin host.workers: (setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-1)); return nil)
    let w = cast[ptr WorkerBox](host.workers[id][0])
    w.inbox.send(readStr(argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32))
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(0))
  nil

proc cbWorkerPost(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_worker_post(data: i32, len: i32): inside a worker, a message to the app that started it.
  when not defined(emscripten):
    if host.workerOut != nil:
      cast[ptr Channel[string]](host.workerOut)[].send(readStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32))
  nil

proc cbWorkerMsg(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_worker_msg(buf: i32, cap: i32) -> i32 (full length): the message being delivered.
  let m = host.workerMsg
  if argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32 > 0: writeStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, m, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32)
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(int32(m.len)))
  nil

proc cbWorkerStop(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_worker_stop(id: i32): the worker finishes what it is doing and ends.
  when not defined(emscripten):
    let id = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
    if id in host.workers:
      let w = cast[ptr WorkerBox](host.workers[id][0])
      w.inbox.send("\0stop")
      host.workers[id] = (host.workers[id][0], -1'i32)   # its thread ends; pumpWorkers frees it
  nil

proc cbCall(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_call(op: i32, text: i32, len: i32) -> i32 (Q11): a voice call; needs the `mic` and
  ## `calls` capabilities (-1 otherwise). ops: 1 call (text "1" to make the offer, "0" to
  ## answer one; then "\n"-separated ICE server URLs), 2 the other side's description
  ## ("type\nsdp"), 3 its candidate ("mid\ncandidate"), 4 hang up, 5 mute ("1") / unmute ("0").
  ## What to send the other side comes back as CallSignal events on the window.
  if "mic" notin currentCaps or "calls" notin currentCaps:
    setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-1))
    return nil
  let op = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  if op < 1 or op > 5: (setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-2)); return nil)
  host.callReq.add (op, readStr(argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32))
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(0))
  nil

proc cbOpenUrl(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_open_url(url: i32, len: i32) -> i32: opens an http(s) or mailto URL with the system
  ## (the browser: OAuth sign-in, links). -1 any other scheme. The engine does it.
  let u = readStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32)
  let l = u.toLowerAscii
  if u.len > 4096 or not (l.startsWith("https://") or l.startsWith("http://") or l.startsWith("mailto:")) or
     u.contains({'\0', '\n', '\r', '"'}):
    setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-1))
    return nil
  host.openReq.add u
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(0))
  nil

proc cbCapture(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_capture(kind: i32, op: i32, name: i32, nameLen: i32) -> i32 (Q2). kind 1 the microphone
  ## (capability `mic`), 2 the camera (`camera`). ops: 1 record into `name` (a file in the
  ## app's data folder: .ogg/.opus/.m4a), 2 pause, 3 resume, 4 stop and keep, 5 cancel and
  ## delete, 6 photo from the camera preview into `name` (.png/.jpg); 7 the level now
  ## (0..1000), 8 the state (0 idle, 1 recording, 2 paused, -1 failed), 9 the length in ms.
  ## -1 refused (capability not declared), -2 a bad name; 0 queued for the engine.
  let kind = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  let op = argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32
  if (kind == 1 and "mic" notin currentCaps) or (kind == 2 and "camera" notin currentCaps) or kind notin [1'i32, 2]:
    setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-1))
    return nil
  case op
  of 7: setResult(results, 0, i32Val(int32(host.captureLevel * 1000)))
  of 8: setResult(results, 0, i32Val(host.captureState))
  of 9: setResult(results, 0, i32Val(host.captureMs))
  of 1 .. 6:
    let name = readStr(argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 3).`of`.i32)
    if op in [1'i32, 6] and (name.len == 0 or name.len > 120 or ".." in name or '/' in name or '\\' in name or ':' in name):
      setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-2))
      return nil
    host.captureReq.add (kind, op, name)
    setResult(results, 0, i32Val(0))
  else: setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-2))
  nil

proc cbFileRead(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## y_file_read(pathPtr: i32, pathLen: i32, outBuf: i32, outCap: i32) -> i32
  if "files" notin currentCaps:
    setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-1))
    return nil
  let pathOffset = argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32
  let nPath = argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32
  let outBuf = argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32
  let outCap = argAt(args, 3).`of`.i32
  let rawPath = readStr(pathOffset, nPath)
  if rawPath.len == 0 or rawPath.contains("..") or rawPath.startsWith("/") or rawPath.startsWith("\\") or (rawPath.len > 1 and rawPath[1] == ':'):
    setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-2))
    return nil
  let fullPath = host.appDir / rawPath   # only inside the app's own folder, never the cwd
  if host.appDir.len == 0 or not fileExists(fullPath):
    setResult(results, 0, i32Val(-3))
    return nil
  let data = readFile(fullPath)
  if outCap == 0:
    setResult(results, 0, i32Val(int32(data.len)))
    return nil
  writeStr(outBuf, data, outCap)
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(int32(min(data.len, int(outCap)))))
  nil

# ===========================================================================
# Import table. This IS the sandbox's entire capability surface.
# ===========================================================================

type
  ImportSpec = object
    params*: seq[WasmValkind]
    results*: seq[WasmValkind]
    cb: WasmFuncCallback

const i32k = WasmValkind(WASM_I32)
const f32k = WasmValkind(WASM_F32)

# --- Vajra apps (built by the Vajra compiler from Atlas cells) ---------------------
# Four more imports: print, stop, and the database through the person using the app.
# The host holds no database itself: the Vajra runtime sets `vajraQueryHook` to run SQL
# as that person (row-level security decides), returning rows joined by \x1f / \x1e, or
# \x15 and the error.
var vajraQueryHook*: proc (sql: string; args: seq[string]): string
# `fetch` the same way: the program that opens the app sets `vajraFetchHook` to the app's
# own rules (host/vajra/livenet.nim: its declared hosts, its secrets); without it the
# app has no network.
var vajraFetchHook*: proc (packed: string): string
var vajraPending {.threadvar.}: string
const vajraExitTrap = "vajra: exit"
proc wasmtimeTrapNew(msg: cstring; len: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.
  importc: "wasmtime_trap_new", header: "<wasmtime.h>".}

proc cbNimWrite(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## nim_write(fd, buf, len) -> len: what a Vajra app prints goes to the app's log.
  host.logs.add readStr(argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32)
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32)); nil

proc cbNimExit(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## nim_exit(code): ends the current call (the app's top-level code ends this way).
  wasmtimeTrapNew(vajraExitTrap, csize_t(vajraExitTrap.len))

proc cbVajraQuery(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## vajra_query(sql, sqlLen, args, argsLen) -> result length (collect with vajra_take).
  let sql = readStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32)
  let packed = readStr(argAt(args, 2).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 3).`of`.i32)
  var params = packed.split('\x1f') # each argument ends with \x1f
  params.setLen max(params.len - 1, 0)
  vajraPending = if vajraQueryHook == nil: "\x15this host has no database"
                 else: vajraQueryHook(sql, params)
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(int32(vajraPending.len))); nil

proc cbVajraFetch(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## vajra_fetch(req, reqLen) -> result length (collect with vajra_take): the app waits.
  let packed = readStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32)
  vajraPending = if vajraFetchHook == nil: "\x15this app has no network here"
                 else: vajraFetchHook(packed)
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(int32(vajraPending.len))); nil

const f64k = WasmValkind(WASM_F64)

proc cbVajraTake(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
  ## vajra_take(dst, cap) -> bytes copied: the result of the last vajra_query.
  let n = min(int(argAt(args, 1).`of`.i32), vajraPending.len)
  writeStr(argAt(args, 0).`of`.i32, vajraPending, int32(n))
  vajraPending = ""
  setResult(results, 0, i32Val(int32(n))); nil

proc cbVajraNow(env: pointer; caller: ptr WasmCaller; args: ptr WasmVal;
    nargs: csize_t; results: ptr WasmVal; nresults: csize_t): ptr WasmTrap {.cdecl.} =
